        ### Constraints
        - Do not introduce a new source: SourceRef|RunRef field on TimelineClip — SD-003 explicitly rejects this. Use the existing source_uuid + generation carry-fields instead.
- Do not reintroduce or extend the parallel placement schema (artagents/core/project/schema.py:build_placement and friends). SD-006 collapses it; sources/ and runs/ directories survive only as local provenance cache.
- Do not import from reigh-app internals. SD-010 mandates building against the public SDK at src/tools/video-editor/index.ts (sprint-08, @publicContract). DataProvider, TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError, fixture constructors come from there.
- Do not put the service-role key on the user-JWT path. SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the AA worker's final timeline-import write. Mirrors reigh-app's SD-022.
- Do not break tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py — byte-equivalent round-trip and allowlist parity with @banodoco/timeline-schema are gating invariants.
- Do not author one-way (AA composes only). SD-012 requires two-way authoring: both reigh-app and AA must be able to write.
- Do not skip the @banodoco/timeline-composition version reconcile. AA's v0.0.5 vs reigh-app sprint-08's local-path v0.0.2 must converge on a shared tag before parity tests are meaningful — flagged as a Phase 1 blocker by the doc.
- Do not silently bypass expected_version. SD-007: timeline-import push retries on conflict with cap N=3, surfaces failure to caller on exhaustion.
- Do not delete sources/ and runs/ directories during the placement-schema collapse. Per SD-006 they remain as local provenance cache; only the placement schema and local timeline.json-as-source-of-truth go.
- Do not lose AA-generated provenance on round-trip. New clips emitted via the worker must populate source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order so reigh-app's TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS allowlist preserves them. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 to AA's local run record for SD-008 drift detection.
- Do not invoke megaplan, the megaplan skill, or nested planning loops — explicit per harness directive.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Honour the SD-011 phase order, but front-load two discovery spikes before code so the worker design isn't rewritten twice. PHASE 0 (~half day, before Phase 1): freeze the @banodoco/timeline-composition version (cut a v0.0.6 tag both AA and reigh-app sprint-08 consume, OR temporarily switch AA to a workspace file:.. path matching reigh-app); resolve open contracts #1 (worker reception model — HTTP push vs poll-queue and ack envelope) and #4 (render lifecycle / realtime channel) by reading delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300 and pollBanodocoTaskStatus, document the answers in a top-of-module docstring on the new worker file. PHASE 1 — Renderer swap: rewrite remotion/src/Root.tsx to a ~30-line file: <Composition id=\"TimelineComposition\" component={TimelineComposition} defaultProps={...} calculateMetadata={({props}) => ({width, height, fps, durationInFrames: getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)})} />, importing from @banodoco/timeline-composition. Delete the AdosCard/AdosLowerThird/CornerLogo helpers — those become theme-package effects. Run remotion smoke; render a hype fixture; compare hash to a reigh-app golden if available, otherwise visual diff. PHASE 2 — Supabase client: add artagents/core/reigh/{env.py,supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py}. env.py extracts _candidate_env_files/_env_first from packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py into a shared helper. supabase_client.py wraps urllib.request POSTs with auth header selection (user JWT vs service-role per call site). data_provider.py implements a Python mirror of the sprint-08 DataProvider interface (fetch, persist, uploadAsset) calling reigh-data-fetch and timeline-import; raise TimelineVersionConflictError analogue on 409 and retry up to N=3 with re-fetch + re-apply (SD-007). Resolve open contract #3 (asset upload contract) by reading the render-outputs RLS migration and DataProvider.uploadAsset signature; pick a single bucket + signed-URL pattern. PHASE 3 — Worker: artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py exposes a polling loop or HTTP endpoint matching the answer from Phase 0 spike. It accepts the BanodocoTaskPayload envelope, dispatches by intent to existing executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc. via the orchestrator registry), writes back via the SupabaseDataProvider with the supplied correlation_id and expected_version, and emits status transitions queued -> running -> complete/failed via the resolved render-lifecycle channel. Add a `worker` subcommand to artagents/pipeline.py. Service-role key gates only the timeline-import write (SD-009). PHASE 4 — CLI collapse: rewrite artagents/core/project/cli.py to expose `list / show <uuid> / edit <uuid>` plus thin wrappers for add-clip / move-clip / set-theme that compose @banodoco/timeline-ops semantics in Python (port the small ops or call out to a node helper). Delete artagents/core/project/{timeline.py,materialize.py} and the placement symbols in schema.py; keep project.py, source.py, run.py for the local provenance cache (sources/, runs/). Update artagents/core/executor/cli.py so --project accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via SupabaseDataProvider. Rewrite tests/test_project_cli.py + delete or rewrite tests/test_project_materialize.py and test_project_schema.py. PHASE 5 — Sequence support: extend the worker to recognize SequenceDraft-shaped tasks; import the schema from banodoco_timeline_schema (or codegen Python types from the Zod source if missing); validate, then materialize via a small Python applySequenceDraftToTimeline equivalent or an out-of-process node call to the sprint-08 sequences module; fall back to an explicit \"unsupported sequence\" failure when the clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY (open contract #5 resolution). PHASE 6 — Parity tests: add tests/test_renderer_parity.py that exports the sprint-08 fixtures (createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture, createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture) to JSON via a small node helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and asserts hash match against a reigh-app golden. Add tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py, tests/test_banodoco_worker.py, tests/test_sequence_support.py for unit coverage. Update tests/test_open_in_reigh.py and tests/test_project_runs.py for SD-008 baseline_snapshot. Throughout, preserve source_uuid/generation/pool_id/clip_order on every clip the worker writes (SD-003) and never reintroduce a structured ref field. Document each open-contract resolution in the module docstring of the file that owns it so future agents see the why.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform: swap AA's Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, implement a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that talks to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read), build the banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes (correlation_id, expected_version, user_jwt) and writes back via timeline-import, collapse AA's CLI to operate on reigh-app project IDs (no more parallel placement schema), support sequence-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema's SequenceDraft, and verify renderer parity using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve the 5 open contracts (worker reception model, JWT+service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor the 12 settled decisions imported from the doc.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform

## Overview

Goal: make ArtAgents (AA) a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform. AA stops being a parallel timeline tool and instead (a) renders via the shared `<TimelineComposition>`, (b) reads/writes the canonical Supabase store via `reigh-data-fetch` + `timeline-import`, (c) hosts a `banodoco` worker that consumes `delegateToBanodocoAgent` envelopes, (d) runs its CLI on reigh-app project UUIDs (parallel placement schema is gone), (e) supports `SequenceDraft`-shaped tasks, and (f) carries renderer-parity tests.

Repository shape that matters:
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx` — 496-line hand-rolled Remotion comp; `@banodoco/timeline-composition` v0.0.5 already installed at `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/timeline-composition/typescript/src/`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}` — local placement schema, CLI, materializer.
- `artagents/timeline.py` — schema authority, re-exports `banodoco_timeline_schema` types.
- `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` — working `reigh-data-fetch` POST client (env discovery + auth).
- `artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py` — file-copy handoff (no real Supabase write yet).
- `artagents/pipeline.py` — top-level dispatcher (extension seam).
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` — test surface to keep/replace.
- `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/timeline-schema/python/banodoco_timeline_schema/` — Python schema package available in-tree.

Hard constraints (per imported decisions):
- SD-001/006: Supabase canonical; collapse placement schema; keep `sources/` + `runs/` only as provenance cache.
- SD-002: renderer must delegate to `<TimelineComposition>`.
- SD-003: never add `source: SourceRef|RunRef`; use existing `source_uuid` + `generation`.
- SD-004/005: implement `banodoco` worker pool; CLI talks via `reigh-data-fetch` + `timeline-import` + `@banodoco/timeline-ops` semantics.
- SD-007: `expected_version` retry, cap N=3.
- SD-008: write `baseline_snapshot` sha256 onto local run records for drift detection.
- SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the worker's final `timeline-import` write.
- SD-010: build against the public SDK surface (`src/tools/video-editor/index.ts`); do not import reigh-app internals.
- SD-012: two-way authoring mandatory.
- Round-trip and allowlist parity tests must keep passing throughout.

This is a large multi-phase plan; phases follow SD-011's ordering with a Phase 0 spike to resolve the open contracts before code that depends on them.

## Phase 0: Discovery & Version Reconcile

### Step 1: Resolve open contracts and pin shared package version (`docs/`, `remotion/package.json`)
**Scope:** Small (research + version pin)
1. **Read** sprint-08's public SDK and delegation client from reigh-app to answer the five open contracts. Required reads (in `../reigh-app` checkout):
   - `src/tools/video-editor/index.ts` — `DataProvider` interface, `TimelineVersionConflictError`, `TimelineNotFoundError`, fixture exports `createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture`/`createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture`.
   - `delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300` — task envelope, `ORCHESTRATOR_TASK_ENQUEUE_URL`, `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
   - The render-outputs Supabase migration + `DataProvider.uploadAsset` signature (asset upload contract).
   - `useTimelinePersistence` retry pattern (mirror for SD-007).
2. **Document** answers to the five open contracts in a single short note (`docs/integration_contracts.md` — new) covering: (1) worker reception model (HTTP push endpoint vs poll-queue) + ack envelope shape, (2) JWT/service-role lifecycle, (3) asset upload contract (bucket name, signed-URL pattern, RLS), (4) render lifecycle channel, (5) sequence schema (whether `SequenceDraft` Python types exist in `banodoco_timeline_schema` or need codegen).
3. **Reconcile** the `@banodoco/timeline-composition` version. AA pins v0.0.5 (GitHub tag) at `remotion/package.json`; reigh-app sprint-08 uses local-path v0.0.2. Pick one of:
   - Cut a fresh `v0.0.6` tag from sprint-08's local copy and bump both consumers, or
   - Switch AA temporarily to `"file:../reigh-app/packages/timeline-composition"` (workspace path) for parity-test work.
   Record the choice in `docs/integration_contracts.md` and update `remotion/package.json` accordingly. **Run** `npm --prefix remotion install` to verify resolution.
4. **Verify** the Python schema package contains what Phase 5 needs by checking `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/timeline-schema/python/banodoco_timeline_schema/__init__.py` and `generated.py` for `SequenceDraft` (or equivalent). If absent, note Phase 5 must shell out to a small node helper or codegen Python types from the Zod source.

## Phase 1: Renderer Swap

### Step 2: Replace hand-rolled Remotion comp with `<TimelineComposition>` (`remotion/src/Root.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `remotion/src/Root.tsx` to a thin `<Composition>` wrapper that imports `TimelineComposition` and `getTimelineDurationInFrames` from `@banodoco/timeline-composition` and forwards `defaultProps`/`calculateMetadata`. Delete the hand-rolled `RenderClip`, `AdosCard`, `AdosLowerThird`, `CornerLogo`, and any local `TimelineClip`/`TimelineConfig`/`AssetRegistry` types — types come from the package.
2. **Pull** the existing `defaultProps` JSON shape forward unchanged (theme, theme_overrides, clips array). `calculateMetadata` should derive `width`, `height`, `fps`, `durationInFrames` via `getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)`.
3. **Remove** any lingering local font/asset helpers that the package now owns. Keep `./fonts` import only if it loads custom fonts the package doesn't ship.
4. **Verify** by running `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` (or the existing `remotion/__smoke__/bundle.mjs` entrypoint) so the bundle compiles. Render an existing fixture (e.g. `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`) headless and eyeball the output.

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + DataProvider (Read & Write)

### Step 3: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move `_read_env_value`, `_candidate_env_files`, `_env_first`, `resolve_api_url`, `resolve_pat` from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105` into `env.py` as the single source of truth.
2. **Add** `resolve_timeline_import_url(...)` and `resolve_service_role_key(...)` companions (analogous lookups: `REIGH_TIMELINE_IMPORT_URL`, `SUPABASE_URL` -> `/functions/v1/timeline-import`; `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`).
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export the moved symbols from `artagents.core.reigh.env` (keep public names stable so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing).

### Step 4: Implement `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring sprint-08's `DataProvider` interface (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py,errors.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError` and `TimelineNotFoundError` (Python analogues of the SDK's named errors).
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin `urllib.request` POST wrapper, takes `endpoint`, `payload`, and an `auth=("user_jwt", token) | ("service_role", key)` selector. Maps HTTP 409 to `TimelineVersionConflictError` and 404 to `TimelineNotFoundError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_import.py` with `push_timeline(*, project_id, timeline_id, timeline, expected_version, auth, retries=3)`. Implements SD-007: on `TimelineVersionConflictError` re-fetch via `reigh-data-fetch`, re-apply the caller's mutation, and retry up to `N=3`; raise on exhaustion. Round-trip the timeline through `artagents.timeline.save_timeline`/`load_timeline`/`validate_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` exposing the methods the sprint-08 `DataProvider` interface defines (per the answer captured in Phase 0): typically `fetch_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> TimelineConfig`, `persist_timeline(...)` (calls `timeline_import.push_timeline`), `upload_asset(local_path | bytes, content_type) -> AssetEntry`. `upload_asset` follows whatever asset upload contract Phase 0 froze (signed URL into the render-outputs/generations bucket).
5. **Auth selection**: `fetch_timeline` and CLI-side `persist_timeline` use the user JWT; the worker's terminal `persist_timeline` call uses the service-role key. Make this explicit by parameterising `auth` rather than reading env mid-call.

### Step 5: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy + SQL-template path with a `SupabaseDataProvider.persist_timeline(...)` call. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` so the default-mode test exercises the mocked `SupabaseDataProvider`; keep `--print-sql`/`--copy-files` test cases.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker

### Step 6: Implement the worker entrypoint (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. The module docstring records the resolution to open contracts #1, #2, #4 (worker reception, JWT lifecycle, render lifecycle).
2. **Implement** the reception model chosen in Phase 0 (HTTP push endpoint or poll-queue loop). The worker accepts the `BanodocoTaskPayload` envelope: `intent`, `brief_inputs`, `theme_id`, `expected_version`, `scope`, `user_jwt`, `project_id`, `timeline_id`, `correlation_id`.
3. **Dispatch** by `intent` to existing AA executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc.) via the orchestrator registry (`artagents/core/orchestrator/`). The executor returns a `TimelineConfig` (or a patch over the fetched one).
4. **Carry-fields**: every clip the worker emits must populate `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003). Compute `baseline_snapshot = sha256(canonical_json(clip))` and persist to the local run record (SD-008).
5. **Persist** results via `SupabaseDataProvider.persist_timeline(...)` using the **service-role** key path (SD-009), passing the supplied `expected_version` and `correlation_id`.
6. **Status updates**: emit `queued -> running -> complete | failed` transitions over whichever channel Phase 0 selected (Realtime channel or status table) so reigh-app's `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` observes them.
7. **Wire** a `worker` (or `banodoco-worker`) subcommand into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help` so `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` runs the loop.

## Phase 4: CLI Collapse — Operate on reigh-app Project UUIDs

### Step 7: Delete the placement schema (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py}`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Delete** `artagents/core/project/timeline.py` and `artagents/core/project/materialize.py` entirely (placement-> TimelineConfig conversion is replaced by fetch + ops + push).
2. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/schema.py`: remove `TIMELINE_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `build_project_timeline`, `build_placement`, `validate_project_timeline`, `validate_placement`, `validate_reference`, `REF_KINDS`, `source_ref`, `run_ref`. Keep only project/source/run schema constants and validators (these still back the local provenance cache).
3. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/project.py`: stop writing `timeline.json` from `create_project`; delete `load_project_timeline` and `save_project_timeline`. Update `show_project` so the timeline summary either fetches via `SupabaseDataProvider` (if a reigh-app `project_id` is mapped) or reports cache-only state.
4. **Keep** `artagents/core/project/source.py`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`, `artagents/core/project/paths.py`, `artagents/core/project/jsonio.py` — provenance cache survives.
5. **Add** an opaque `project_id` field on the local `project.json` that maps the local slug to its reigh-app UUID (so subsequent CLI verbs resolve UUIDs from a slug if needed).

### Step 8: Rewrite the `projects` CLI surface (`artagents/core/project/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `artagents/core/project/cli.py` so subcommands are: `create`, `show`, `source add`, `list <project_id>`, `edit <project_id>`. Drop `timeline place-source`, `timeline place-run`, `timeline remove`, `materialize`.
2. **Implement** `show` and `list`/`edit` against `SupabaseDataProvider` (read via `reigh-data-fetch`, write via `timeline-import` with the user JWT). Edit verbs (`add-clip`, `move-clip`, `set-theme`) compose `@banodoco/timeline-ops` semantics — port the small set of ops to Python in `artagents/core/reigh/ops.py`, OR shell out to a small node helper that imports the published package and emits a patched timeline. Pick whichever the Phase 0 spike confirms is cheapest.
3. **Wire** SD-007 retry: every `edit` mutation goes through `timeline_import.push_timeline(..., retries=3)`.
4. **Update** `artagents/core/executor/cli.py:_cmd_run` so `--project` accepts a reigh-app project UUID and writes results back via `SupabaseDataProvider`. Keep the slug path as cache-only/offline mode.

### Step 9: Rewrite project-CLI tests (`tests/test_project_cli.py`, `tests/test_project_materialize.py`, `tests/test_project_schema.py`, `tests/test_project_runs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_create_show_source_place_materialize_and_remove` (currently asserts placement flow). Replace with: create -> source add -> mocked `reigh-data-fetch` for `show`/`list` -> mocked `timeline-import` for `edit`, asserting `expected_version` retry on a forced 409.
2. **Delete** `tests/test_project_materialize.py` (placement-only) or rewrite minimal coverage as fetch+ops+push.
3. **Trim** `tests/test_project_schema.py` to project/source/run schema only.
4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to cover the new `baseline_snapshot` sha256 field on run records (SD-008).

## Phase 5: Sequence Support

### Step 10: Worker handling for `SequenceDraft`-shaped tasks (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py` that imports `SequenceDraft` from `banodoco_timeline_schema` if present (Phase 0 confirmed availability), otherwise wraps a small node helper that imports `@banodoco/timeline-schema` and validates payloads.
2. **Detect** sequence-shaped intents in the worker: when the inbound task carries a `SequenceDraft` (or its `clipType` lives in `LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY` / `THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY`), validate, then materialise via a small `apply_sequence_draft_to_timeline(...)` (Python port or out-of-process node call).
3. **Fail closed** with an explicit `unsupported sequence: <clipType>` error envelope when the clip type is not in the registries.

## Phase 6: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 11: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock `urllib.request` to a fake Supabase endpoint; assert (a) `fetch_timeline` round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `persist_timeline` retries on 409 with cap N=3 and surfaces failure on exhaustion, (c) auth header selection differs between user-JWT and service-role calls.
2. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake `BanodocoTaskPayload` envelope; assert intent dispatch, `correlation_id` echo, `expected_version` propagation, status transitions, and that emitted clips carry `source_uuid`/`generation`/`pool_id`/`clip_order` plus a `baseline_snapshot` on the local run record.
3. **Add** `tests/test_sequence_support.py`: a `SequenceDraft`-shaped task with a registered `clipType` materialises; an unregistered `clipType` surfaces `unsupported sequence`.

### Step 12: Renderer parity smoke (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `tests/fixtures/sprint08/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** a small node helper at `tests/fixtures/sprint08/export_fixtures.mjs` that imports `createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture` and `createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture` from the public SDK (`@banodoco/...`/`reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/index.ts`) and emits JSON.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells out to the helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and compares hash against a `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/` snapshot exported from reigh-app. Mark the test `@pytest.mark.skipif` when the helper or goldens are missing so CI without sprint-08 still passes.

## Execution Order

1. Phase 0 — version reconcile + open-contract resolution (blocks Phase 1's parity meaning and Phase 3's worker shape).
2. Phase 1 — renderer swap (small, isolated; unblocks parity tests).
3. Phase 2 — Supabase client + `SupabaseDataProvider` + retry (blocks Phases 3/4/6).
4. Phase 3 — banodoco worker (uses Phase 2; required by reigh-app's delegation pathway).
5. Phase 4 — CLI collapse (uses Phase 2; deletes parallel schema + rewrites tests).
6. Phase 5 — sequence support (extends Phase 3 worker).
7. Phase 6 — parity + new unit tests (validates everything above).

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end `delegateToBanodocoAgent` -> AA worker -> `timeline-import` flow against a staging reigh-app.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py` (the deleted/rewritten suite).
5. After Phase 5: `pytest tests/test_sequence_support.py`.
6. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform across six phases: (1) swap AA's hand-rolled Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, (2) build a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that POSTs to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read) with expected_version retry, (3) build a banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes and writes back via timeline-import, (4) collapse AA's parallel placement schema/CLI so verbs operate on reigh-app project IDs, (5) support SequenceDraft-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema, (6) add renderer parity tests using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve five open contracts (worker reception model, JWT/service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor 12 settled decisions from .megaplan/epic_bidirectional_editing.md.

        ### Key Evidence
        - AA's renderer is a 496-line hand-rolled <TimelineComposition> in remotion/src/Root.tsx that ignores the already-installed @banodoco/timeline-composition v0.0.5 package. The package exports a real <TimelineComposition> from typescript/src/TimelineComposition.tsx with EFFECT_REGISTRY/CLIP_TYPE_ALIASES dispatch, ThemeProvider, AudioTrack/VisualClip, and getTimelineDurationInFrames — the swap drops the hand-rolled clip rendering and delegates entirely. (source: remotion/src/Root.tsx; remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/timeline-composition/typescript/src/{index.ts,TimelineComposition.tsx}; remotion/package.json; relevance: high)
- Version reconcile is required before parity tests are meaningful: AA pins @banodoco/timeline-composition v0.0.5 (GitHub archive tag); reigh-app sprint-08 uses a local file path at v0.0.2. Brief explicitly flags this — must ship a fresh tag both sides consume or AA reverts to local path during dev.         ### Test Expectations
        - [pass_to_pass] remotion/__smoke__/bundle.mjs (smoke): Remotion bundle compiles after Root.tsx is reduced to a <TimelineComposition> delegation. Run `npm -w remotion run smoke` after Phase 1.

- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_banodoco_worker.py (new): New tests for the banodoco worker: accepts BanodocoTaskPayload envelope (intent, brief_inputs, theme_id, expected_version, scope, user_jwt, project_id, timeline_id, correlation_id), dispatches by intent, surfaces status updates the orchestrator can poll.

        ### Constraints
        - Do not introduce a new source: SourceRef|RunRef field on TimelineClip — SD-003 explicitly rejects this. Use the existing source_uuid + generation carry-fields instead.
- Do not reintroduce or extend the parallel placement schema (artagents/core/project/schema.py:build_placement and friends). SD-006 collapses it; sources/ and runs/ directories survive only as local provenance cache.
- Do not import from reigh-app internals. SD-010 mandates building against the public SDK at src/tools/video-editor/index.ts (sprint-08, @publicContract). DataProvider, TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError, fixture constructors come from there.
- Do not put the service-role key on the user-JWT path. SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the AA worker's final timeline-import write. Mirrors reigh-app's SD-022.
- Do not break tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py — byte-equivalent round-trip and allowlist parity with @banodoco/timeline-schema are gating invariants.
- Do not author one-way (AA composes only). SD-012 requires two-way authoring: both reigh-app and AA must be able to write.
- Do not skip the @banodoco/timeline-composition version reconcile. AA's v0.0.5 vs reigh-app sprint-08's local-path v0.0.2 must converge on a shared tag before parity tests are meaningful — flagged as a Phase 1 blocker by the doc.
- Do not silently bypass expected_version. SD-007: timeline-import push retries on conflict with cap N=3, surfaces failure to caller on exhaustion.
- Do not delete sources/ and runs/ directories during the placement-schema collapse. Per SD-006 they remain as local provenance cache; only the placement schema and local timeline.json-as-source-of-truth go.
- Do not lose AA-generated provenance on round-trip. New clips emitted via the worker must populate source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order so reigh-app's TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS allowlist preserves them. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 to AA's local run record for SD-008 drift detection.
- Do not invoke megaplan, the megaplan skill, or nested planning loops — explicit per harness directive.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Honour the SD-011 phase order, but front-load two discovery spikes before code so the worker design isn't rewritten twice. PHASE 0 (~half day, before Phase 1): freeze the @banodoco/timeline-composition version (cut a v0.0.6 tag both AA and reigh-app sprint-08 consume, OR temporarily switch AA to a workspace file:.. path matching reigh-app); resolve open contracts #1 (worker reception model — HTTP push vs poll-queue and ack envelope) and #4 (render lifecycle / realtime channel) by reading delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300 and pollBanodocoTaskStatus, document the answers in a top-of-module docstring on the new worker file. PHASE 1 — Renderer swap: rewrite remotion/src/Root.tsx to a ~30-line file: <Composition id=\"TimelineComposition\" component={TimelineComposition} defaultProps={...} calculateMetadata={({props}) => ({width, height, fps, durationInFrames: getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)})} />, importing from @banodoco/timeline-composition. Delete the AdosCard/AdosLowerThird/CornerLogo helpers — those become theme-package effects. Run remotion smoke; render a hype fixture; compare hash to a reigh-app golden if available, otherwise visual diff. PHASE 2 — Supabase client: add artagents/core/reigh/{env.py,supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py}. env.py extracts _candidate_env_files/_env_first from packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py into a shared helper. supabase_client.py wraps urllib.request POSTs with auth header selection (user JWT vs service-role per call site). data_provider.py implements a Python mirror of the sprint-08 DataProvider interface (fetch, persist, uploadAsset) calling reigh-data-fetch and timeline-import; raise TimelineVersionConflictError analogue on 409 and retry up to N=3 with re-fetch + re-apply (SD-007). Resolve open contract #3 (asset upload contract) by reading the render-outputs RLS migration and DataProvider.uploadAsset signature; pick a single bucket + signed-URL pattern. PHASE 3 — Worker: artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py exposes a polling loop or HTTP endpoint matching the answer from Phase 0 spike. It accepts the BanodocoTaskPayload envelope, dispatches by intent to existing executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc. via the orchestrator registry), writes back via the SupabaseDataProvider with the supplied correlation_id and expected_version, and emits status transitions queued -> running -> complete/failed via the resolved render-lifecycle channel. Add a `worker` subcommand to artagents/pipeline.py. Service-role key gates only the timeline-import write (SD-009). PHASE 4 — CLI collapse: rewrite artagents/core/project/cli.py to expose `list / show <uuid> / edit <uuid>` plus thin wrappers for add-clip / move-clip / set-theme that compose @banodoco/timeline-ops semantics in Python (port the small ops or call out to a node helper). Delete artagents/core/project/{timeline.py,materialize.py} and the placement symbols in schema.py; keep project.py, source.py, run.py for the local provenance cache (sources/, runs/). Update artagents/core/executor/cli.py so --project accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via SupabaseDataProvider. Rewrite tests/test_project_cli.py + delete or rewrite tests/test_project_materialize.py and test_project_schema.py. PHASE 5 — Sequence support: extend the worker to recognize SequenceDraft-shaped tasks; import the schema from banodoco_timeline_schema (or codegen Python types from the Zod source if missing); validate, then materialize via a small Python applySequenceDraftToTimeline equivalent or an out-of-process node call to the sprint-08 sequences module; fall back to an explicit \"unsupported sequence\" failure when the clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY (open contract #5 resolution). PHASE 6 — Parity tests: add tests/test_renderer_parity.py that exports the sprint-08 fixtures (createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture, createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture) to JSON via a small node helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and asserts hash match against a reigh-app golden. Add tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py, tests/test_banodoco_worker.py, tests/test_sequence_support.py for unit coverage. Update tests/test_open_in_reigh.py and tests/test_project_runs.py for SD-008 baseline_snapshot. Throughout, preserve source_uuid/generation/pool_id/clip_order on every clip the worker writes (SD-003) and never reintroduce a structured ref field. Document each open-contract resolution in the module docstring of the file that owns it so future agents see the why.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform: swap AA's Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, implement a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that talks to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read), build the banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes (correlation_id, expected_version, user_jwt) and writes back via timeline-import, collapse AA's CLI to operate on reigh-app project IDs (no more parallel placement schema), support sequence-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema's SequenceDraft, and verify renderer parity using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve the 5 open contracts (worker reception model, JWT+service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor the 12 settled decisions imported from the doc.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform (v2)

## Overview

Goal unchanged: make ArtAgents (AA) a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform — renderer delegation to `<TimelineComposition>`, a Python `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the public TS surface, a `banodoco`-pool worker that conforms to the orchestrator claim/complete protocol, CLI collapse onto reigh-app project UUIDs, sequence-draft support, and renderer parity tests.

This v2 revision re-grounds every mechanism against the actual reigh-app sources cited by the gate (`../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/{index.ts,testing.ts,sequence.ts,data/DataProvider.ts,data/SupabaseDataProvider.ts}`, `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/{timeline-import,reigh-data-fetch,task-status}/index.ts`, `../reigh-app/.../delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts`). Concrete corrections folded in:

- Worker auth: `timeline-import` rejects service-role-only callers (`401: user JWT required (service-role not accepted on this path)`). AA's worker writes with a short-lived user JWT delivered by the orchestrator's claim response (bound to the project owner). SD-009's intent — "service-role power is gated to the worker" — survives, but the mechanism is JWT-via-orchestrator, not bare service-role.
- Retry: `save_timeline` takes a mutator callback `(timeline, version) -> timeline`; on 409 the loop re-fetches, re-applies the mutator against the fresh config+version, retries up to N=3.
- Read contract: extend `TIMELINES_SELECT` in `reigh-data-fetch` to include `config_version` (a small reigh-app supporting change scoped into Phase 0).
- Asset upload: bucket `timeline-assets`, path `${userId}/${timelineId}/${Date.now()}-${filename}`, then `registerAsset` writes the registry entry — mirrors `SupabaseDataProvider.ts`.
- DataProvider surface: pin exact method names — `loadTimeline`, `saveTimeline`, `loadAssetRegistry`, `resolveAssetUrl`, plus optional `registerAsset`, `uploadAsset`, `loadCheckpoints`/`saveCheckpoint`, `loadWaveform`/`saveWaveform`, `loadProfile`/`saveProfile` — covering everything `data/DataProvider.ts` advertises.
- Worker reception: AA conforms to the orchestrator's claim/complete/status protocol (`worker_pool='banodoco'` claim endpoint → execute → complete endpoint → `task-status` row that `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reads from `/functions/v1/task-status?task_id=...`). No arbitrary HTTP push, no fictional poll loop.
- Envelope handling: worker honours optional `current_timeline` snapshot in the envelope (skip fetch when present); tests cover with-snapshot and without-snapshot.
- `expected_version`: Python client requires it for normal writes; `force=True` is the only opt-in for last-write-wins. Edge-function permissiveness is no excuse.
- Sequence contract: use the public `validateSequenceDraft` / `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` / `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_*` API from `sequence.ts`. Drop the made-up `LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY` / `THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY` references.
- Timeline ops: never port to Python. AA shells to a node helper backed by `createTimelineCommandRunner` (re-exported from the public SDK).
- Package versions: reconcile `@banodoco/timeline-composition`, `@banodoco/timeline-schema`, AND `@banodoco/timeline-ops` together. All three are local file deps in reigh-app; AA pins all three.
- Fixture imports: `createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture` / `createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture` come from `src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts` (not `index.ts`).
- `baseline_snapshot` storage: pinned at `runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot` (sha256 hex). `metadata` already accepts arbitrary keys (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,run.py}`).

Repository shape that matters (unchanged from v1):
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx`, `remotion/package.json`, `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/{timeline-composition,timeline-schema}/**`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}`.
- `artagents/timeline.py`.
- `artagents/packs/builtin/{reigh_data,open_in_reigh}/run.py`.
- `artagents/pipeline.py`.
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py`.

Hard constraints from imported decisions are unchanged; only mechanisms behind SD-007 / SD-008 / SD-009 are re-pointed at the actual reigh-app contracts.

## Phase 0: Discovery, Cross-Repo Supporting Changes, Version Reconcile

### Step 1: Capture cross-repo contracts and write the integration brief (`docs/integration_contracts.md`)
**Scope:** Small (research + doc)
1. **Read** the canonical reigh-app surfaces and quote line ranges into `docs/integration_contracts.md` (new):
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/DataProvider.ts` — full method list (`loadTimeline`, `saveTimeline`, `loadAssetRegistry`, `resolveAssetUrl`, optional `registerAsset`/`uploadAsset`/checkpoint/waveform/profile).
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/SupabaseDataProvider.ts` — bucket name (`timeline-assets`), path layout, `registerAsset` semantics, `expected_version` flow.
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/index.ts` and `testing.ts` — confirm fixture exports live in `testing.ts` only.
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/sequence.ts` — `validateSequenceDraft`, `applySequenceDraftToTimeline`, `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_*`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/timeline-import/index.ts` — auth gate (`!auth?.userId -> 401 user JWT required (service-role not accepted on this path)`), `expected_version` semantics.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` — `TIMELINES_SELECT` columns, lack of `config_version`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/task-status/index.ts` and `delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts` — claim/complete/status protocol, envelope shape (`task_type`, `params`, `project_id`, `run_type`, `worker_pool`, optional `current_timeline`), polling target (`/functions/v1/task-status?task_id=...`).
2. **Document** in `docs/integration_contracts.md` the resolutions for the five open contracts:
   - **#1 Worker reception** — orchestrator claim/complete/status protocol; AA polls the orchestrator's claim endpoint for `worker_pool='banodoco'`, calls the complete endpoint when done, and `task-status` is the polling surface reigh-app sees.
   - **#2 JWT/service-role lifecycle** — orchestrator's claim response includes a short-lived user JWT scoped to the project owner; AA uses that JWT for the terminal `timeline-import` write. Service-role key is used only for orchestrator-internal calls AA makes, never against `timeline-import`.
   - **#3 Asset upload** — `timeline-assets` bucket, path `${userId}/${timelineId}/${Date.now()}-${filename}`, then `registerAsset` writes the registry entry.
   - **#4 Render lifecycle** — orchestrator complete endpoint updates the row `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reads; no separate Realtime channel for v1.
   - **#5 Sequence schema** — TS-only public API in `sequence.ts`; AA shells to a node helper for `validateSequenceDraft` + `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` until Python types ship.
3. **Reconcile** all three shared packages in `remotion/package.json`. AA pins matching versions of `@banodoco/timeline-composition`, `@banodoco/timeline-schema`, AND `@banodoco/timeline-ops`. Pick one of two strategies and record it in the doc:
   - Cut a coordinated tag bump for all three and consume them in both repos, or
   - Switch AA to `file:` paths matching reigh-app's local-file workspace layout for the dev cycle.
   Run `npm --prefix remotion install` after the bump.

### Step 2: Reigh-app supporting changes (`../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts`, optional `task-status` doc)
**Scope:** Small (cross-repo PR)
1. **Extend** `TIMELINES_SELECT` in `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` to include `config_version`. Bump the function version stamp per repo convention. Without this, `expected_version` writes from AA cannot work.
2. **Confirm** (via doc patch in the same PR if needed) that the orchestrator's claim response surfaces a user-JWT bound to the project owner for `worker_pool='banodoco'` claims. If not, add that field as part of this PR — Phase 3 depends on it.
3. **Open** the cross-repo PR; track its merge state in `docs/integration_contracts.md`. AA's Phase 3 work is gated on this merging.

## Phase 1: Renderer Swap

### Step 3: Replace hand-rolled Remotion comp with `<TimelineComposition>` (`remotion/src/Root.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `remotion/src/Root.tsx` to a thin `<Composition>` wrapper that imports `TimelineComposition` and `getTimelineDurationInFrames` from `@banodoco/timeline-composition`. Forward `defaultProps`/`calculateMetadata`. Delete local `RenderClip`, `AdosCard`, `AdosLowerThird`, `CornerLogo`, and local `TimelineClip`/`TimelineConfig`/`AssetRegistry` types.
2. **Pull** the existing `defaultProps` JSON shape forward unchanged. `calculateMetadata` derives `width`/`height`/`fps`/`durationInFrames` via `getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)`.
3. **Keep** `./fonts` only if it loads custom fonts the package doesn't ship.
4. **Verify** with `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` (or `node remotion/__smoke__/bundle.mjs`); render `examples/hype.timeline.full.json` headless.

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + Full DataProvider Surface

### Step 4: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move `_read_env_value`, `_candidate_env_files`, `_env_first`, `resolve_api_url`, `resolve_pat` from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105` into `env.py`.
2. **Add** `resolve_timeline_import_url(...)`, `resolve_orchestrator_url(...)`, and (only for orchestrator-internal calls — never `timeline-import`) `resolve_service_role_key(...)`.
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export from `artagents.core.reigh.env` so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing.

### Step 5: Implement Python `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the canonical TS surface (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_io.py,errors.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError` and `TimelineNotFoundError` (mapped from HTTP 409 / 404).
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin `urllib.request` POST wrapper. Auth selector parameterised: `auth=("user_jwt", token)` (default) or `auth=("service_role", key)` (only for non-`timeline-import` endpoints). Maps 409 → `TimelineVersionConflictError`, 404 → `TimelineNotFoundError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_io.py` with the mutator-based save:

   ```python
   def save_timeline(
       *,
       project_id: str,
       timeline_id: str,
       mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig],
       auth: Auth,
       retries: int = 3,
       force: bool = False,
   ) -> SaveResult: ...
   ```

   Loop: `(timeline, version) = load_timeline(...)`; `next_timeline = mutator(timeline, version)`; POST to `timeline-import` with `expected_version=version` (or omit only when `force=True`). On `TimelineVersionConflictError`, re-fetch and re-apply the mutator against the fresh `(timeline, version)` until `retries` exhausts; raise on exhaustion. Reject `expected_version=None` in the normal path — `force=True` is the only legitimate bypass and is logged.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` exposing the exact sprint-08 method set (Python snake_case bound to TS camelCase via the docstring; mirrors `data/DataProvider.ts`):
   - `load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> tuple[TimelineConfig, int]` (returns config + `config_version`).
   - `save_timeline(project_id, timeline_id, mutator, auth, retries=3, force=False)` — wraps `timeline_io.save_timeline`.
   - `load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id) -> AssetRegistry`.
   - `resolve_asset_url(asset_entry) -> str` (mirror `AssetResolver`).
   - Optional: `register_asset(project_id, timeline_id, asset_entry)`, `upload_asset(local_path_or_bytes, *, content_type, user_id, timeline_id, filename) -> AssetEntry` (uploads to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then `register_asset`), `load_checkpoints` / `save_checkpoint`, `load_waveform` / `save_waveform`, `load_profile` / `save_profile`.
5. **Round-trip** every fetched timeline through `artagents.timeline.validate_timeline` / `load_timeline` / `save_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.
6. **Auth selection is explicit and parameter-driven**. CLI verbs pass the user JWT they got from env (PAT) or workflow harness; the worker passes the orchestrator-issued short-lived user JWT. No code path silently uses the service-role key against `timeline-import`.

### Step 6: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy path with a `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., mutator=lambda t, v: merged_timeline)` call. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` so the default path mocks the DataProvider; keep escape-hatch test cases.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker (Orchestrator Claim/Complete Protocol)

### Step 7: Implement worker conforming to orchestrator protocol (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. Module docstring records resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with citations to `../reigh-app/.../delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts` and `task-status/index.ts`.
2. **Reception model**: AA's worker is a long-running poll loop hitting the orchestrator's claim endpoint for `worker_pool='banodoco'` (URL from `resolve_orchestrator_url`). The claim response carries the `BanodocoTaskPayload` envelope (`task_type='banodoco_timeline_generate'`, `params={ intent, brief_inputs, theme_id, expected_version, scope, project_id, timeline_id, correlation_id, user_jwt, current_timeline? }`, `project_id`, `run_type='banodoco-worker'`) and a short-lived `user_jwt`.
3. **Snapshot fast-path**: if `params.current_timeline` is present, dispatch using it directly (no extra `load_timeline` round-trip). Otherwise call `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id)`.
4. **Dispatch by `intent`** to existing AA executors via the orchestrator registry under `artagents/core/orchestrator/`. Each executor returns a mutator callable so `save_timeline`'s retry loop can re-apply against fresh state.
5. **Carry-fields**: every clip the executor emits populates `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003).
6. **Local provenance**: write a run record at `runs/<run_id>.json` with `metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload))` (SD-008). Storage path is fixed at `runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot`.
7. **Persist**: call `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(project_id, timeline_id, mutator, auth=("user_jwt", task.user_jwt), retries=3)`. `expected_version=params.expected_version` is required; `force=True` is forbidden on this path.
8. **Complete the task** by calling the orchestrator's complete endpoint with the result (success → status `complete`, failure → status `failed` plus error details), so the row `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reads from `/functions/v1/task-status?task_id=...` reflects the right state. No direct writes to a `tasks` table from AA.
9. **Wire** `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help`.

## Phase 4: CLI Collapse — Operate on reigh-app Project UUIDs

### Step 8: Delete the placement schema (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,project.py}`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Delete** `artagents/core/project/timeline.py` and `artagents/core/project/materialize.py`.
2. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/schema.py`: remove `TIMELINE_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `build_project_timeline`, `build_placement`, `validate_project_timeline`, `validate_placement`, `validate_reference`, `REF_KINDS`, `source_ref`, `run_ref`. Keep project/source/run schema constants and validators.
3. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/project.py`: stop writing `timeline.json` from `create_project`; delete `load_project_timeline`/`save_project_timeline`. `show_project` either fetches via `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline` (if `project.json#project_id` is set) or reports cache-only state.
4. **Keep** `source.py`, `run.py`, `paths.py`, `jsonio.py`. Add an opaque `project_id` field on `project.json` mapping local slug → reigh-app UUID.

### Step 9: Rewrite the `projects` CLI surface using a node `timeline-ops` helper (`artagents/core/project/cli.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/ops_helper.mjs`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `artagents/core/project/cli.py` so subcommands are: `create`, `show`, `source add`, `list <project_id>`, `edit <project_id>`. Drop placement verbs.
2. **Edit verbs** (`add-clip`, `move-clip`, `set-theme`) shell out to a small node helper at `artagents/core/reigh/ops_helper.mjs` that imports `createTimelineCommandRunner` from the public SDK and applies the requested command list to a timeline JSON read from stdin, emitting the result JSON. The Python CLI builds the command list, hands it to the helper, and treats the helper's output as the mutator's return value. Do not port timeline-ops to Python.
3. **Wire** SD-007 retry: every `edit` mutation goes through `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., mutator=run_ops_helper(commands), retries=3)`. `expected_version` is required; CLI never silently force-saves.
4. **Update** `artagents/core/executor/cli.py:_cmd_run` so `--project` accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via `SupabaseDataProvider`. Keep the slug path as cache-only/offline mode.

### Step 10: Rewrite project-CLI tests (`tests/test_project_cli.py`, `tests/test_project_materialize.py`, `tests/test_project_schema.py`, `tests/test_project_runs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_*` against mocked `reigh-data-fetch` + `timeline-import` (force a 409 and assert the mutator is re-applied; assert exhaustion at N=3; assert that omitting `expected_version` raises rather than force-saving).
2. **Delete** `tests/test_project_materialize.py` (placement-only) or rewrite minimal coverage.
3. **Trim** `tests/test_project_schema.py` to project/source/run schema only.
4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to assert `metadata.baseline_snapshot` is a sha256 hex string at exactly that path on the run record (SD-008).

## Phase 5: Sequence Support via Public `sequence.ts` API

### Step 11: Worker handling for `SequenceDraft` tasks via the public API (`artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequence_helper.mjs`, `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/sequence_helper.mjs` — a node helper that imports `validateSequenceDraft`, `applySequenceDraftToTimeline`, and `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` (and any related `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_*` metadata) from the public SDK re-export at `src/tools/video-editor/sequence.ts` (or `@banodoco/timeline-schema`'s public sequence export when available).
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py` that shells to the helper to validate a `SequenceDraft` and apply it to a timeline. It returns a mutator usable by `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline`.
3. **Detect** sequence-shaped intents in the worker: when `params.intent` carries a `SequenceDraft`, run `sequences.validate(...)`. If the `clipType` is not in `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES`, fail closed with `unsupported sequence: <clipType>` and complete the task as `failed`.

## Phase 6: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 12: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert (a) `load_timeline` returns `(config, config_version)` and round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `save_timeline` re-applies the mutator on 409 and exhausts at N=3, (c) `save_timeline` raises when `expected_version` is omitted unless `force=True`, (d) auth header differs between user-JWT and service-role calls and `timeline-import` only ever sees user-JWT, (e) `upload_asset` writes to `timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` and follows up with `register_asset`.
2. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake claim response. Assert intent dispatch, `correlation_id` echo, `expected_version` propagation, snapshot fast-path with and without `current_timeline`, status transitions via the orchestrator complete endpoint (not direct table writes), `source_uuid`/`generation`/`pool_id`/`clip_order` on emitted clips, and `metadata.baseline_snapshot` at `runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot`.
3. **Add** `tests/test_sequence_support.py`: a `SequenceDraft` with a `clipType` in `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` materialises through the helper; an unrecognised `clipType` surfaces `unsupported sequence` and completes the task as `failed`.

### Step 13: Renderer parity smoke importing fixtures from `testing.ts` (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `tests/fixtures/sprint08/export_fixtures.mjs`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `tests/fixtures/sprint08/export_fixtures.mjs` that imports `createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture` and `createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture` from `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts` (or the package subpath that re-exports `testing.ts`). Not from `index.ts`.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells to the helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, compares hash to `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/`. Skip with a documented reason if the helper or goldens are unavailable.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** — contract resolution + `config_version` PR + version reconcile (blocks Phase 2/3/6).
2. **Phase 1** — renderer swap (isolated; unblocks parity tests).
3. **Phase 2** — DataProvider with full surface, mutator-based retry, asset upload to `timeline-assets`.
4. **Phase 3** — orchestrator-protocol worker (gated on Phase 0 PR landing).
5. **Phase 4** — CLI collapse + node ops helper.
6. **Phase 5** — sequence support via node helper over `sequence.ts`.
7. **Phase 6** — parity + new unit tests.

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end claim → execute → complete → `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` flow against staging reigh-app.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py`.
5. After Phase 5: `pytest tests/test_sequence_support.py`.
6. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.
