- [pass_to_pass] tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py::TimelineRoundTripFixtureTest::test_round_trip_is_byte_equivalent: examples/hype.timeline.full.json survives a load/save round-trip byte-equivalent. Must keep passing through the schema collapse and Supabase client work.
- [pass_to_pass] tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py::TimelineRoundTripFixtureTest::test_allowlist_parity_with_shared_schema: AA's _TIMELINE_TOP_ALLOWED / _CLIP_ALLOWED / _TRACK_ALLOWED match @banodoco/timeline-schema's JSON Schema. Adding sequence-aware fields must not break parity.
- [pass_to_pass] tests/test_reigh_data.py: reigh-data-fetch client semantics. Stays green as the SupabaseDataProvider extends it.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_create_show_source_place_materialize_and_remove: Currently asserts the placement-style flow that SD-006 collapses. Test must be rewritten to assert the new reigh-app-backed CLI verbs (list/show/edit via mocked reigh-data-fetch + timeline-import) with expected_version retry coverage.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_project_materialize.py: Placement -> TimelineConfig materialize. Deleted or rewritten as fetch+ops+push.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_project_schema.py: Placement schema validation. Reduced to project/source/run schema tests.
- [pass_to_pass] tests/test_project_runs.py: Run record provenance schema. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 coverage (SD-008) without breaking existing assertions.
- [pass_to_pass] tests/test_open_in_reigh.py: File-copy and --print-sql escape-hatch paths. Stay green; add coverage that the default flow now pushes via timeline-import.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py (new): New unit tests for SupabaseDataProvider: fetch via reigh-data-fetch, push via timeline-import, expected_version conflict retry (cap N=3), JWT vs service-role header selection.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_banodoco_worker.py (new): New tests for the banodoco worker: accepts BanodocoTaskPayload envelope (intent, brief_inputs, theme_id, expected_version, scope, user_jwt, project_id, timeline_id, correlation_id), dispatches by intent, surfaces status updates the orchestrator can poll.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_sequence_support.py (new): Worker handles SequenceDraft-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema validation; falls back to 'unsupported sequence' when clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY.
- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_renderer_parity.py (new): Renderer parity smoke: uses createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture / createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture (or a JSON export of them) to render via AA's Remotion entry; compares hash to a reigh-app golden.

        ### Constraints
        - Do not introduce a new source: SourceRef|RunRef field on TimelineClip — SD-003 explicitly rejects this. Use the existing source_uuid + generation carry-fields instead.
- Do not reintroduce or extend the parallel placement schema (artagents/core/project/schema.py:build_placement and friends). SD-006 collapses it; sources/ and runs/ directories survive only as local provenance cache.
- Do not import from reigh-app internals. SD-010 mandates building against the public SDK at src/tools/video-editor/index.ts (sprint-08, @publicContract). DataProvider, TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError, fixture constructors come from there.
- Do not put the service-role key on the user-JWT path. SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the AA worker's final timeline-import write. Mirrors reigh-app's SD-022.
- Do not break tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py — byte-equivalent round-trip and allowlist parity with @banodoco/timeline-schema are gating invariants.
- Do not author one-way (AA composes only). SD-012 requires two-way authoring: both reigh-app and AA must be able to write.
- Do not skip the @banodoco/timeline-composition version reconcile. AA's v0.0.5 vs reigh-app sprint-08's local-path v0.0.2 must converge on a shared tag before parity tests are meaningful — flagged as a Phase 1 blocker by the doc.
- Do not silently bypass expected_version. SD-007: timeline-import push retries on conflict with cap N=3, surfaces failure to caller on exhaustion.
- Do not delete sources/ and runs/ directories during the placement-schema collapse. Per SD-006 they remain as local provenance cache; only the placement schema and local timeline.json-as-source-of-truth go.
- Do not lose AA-generated provenance on round-trip. New clips emitted via the worker must populate source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order so reigh-app's TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS allowlist preserves them. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 to AA's local run record for SD-008 drift detection.
- Do not invoke megaplan, the megaplan skill, or nested planning loops — explicit per harness directive.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Honour the SD-011 phase order, but front-load two discovery spikes before code so the worker design isn't rewritten twice. PHASE 0 (~half day, before Phase 1): freeze the @banodoco/timeline-composition version (cut a v0.0.6 tag both AA and reigh-app sprint-08 consume, OR temporarily switch AA to a workspace file:.. path matching reigh-app); resolve open contracts #1 (worker reception model — HTTP push vs poll-queue and ack envelope) and #4 (render lifecycle / realtime channel) by reading delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300 and pollBanodocoTaskStatus, document the answers in a top-of-module docstring on the new worker file. PHASE 1 — Renderer swap: rewrite remotion/src/Root.tsx to a ~30-line file: <Composition id=\"TimelineComposition\" component={TimelineComposition} defaultProps={...} calculateMetadata={({props}) => ({width, height, fps, durationInFrames: getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)})} />, importing from @banodoco/timeline-composition. Delete the AdosCard/AdosLowerThird/CornerLogo helpers — those become theme-package effects. Run remotion smoke; render a hype fixture; compare hash to a reigh-app golden if available, otherwise visual diff. PHASE 2 — Supabase client: add artagents/core/reigh/{env.py,supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py}. env.py extracts _candidate_env_files/_env_first from packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py into a shared helper. supabase_client.py wraps urllib.request POSTs with auth header selection (user JWT vs service-role per call site). data_provider.py implements a Python mirror of the sprint-08 DataProvider interface (fetch, persist, uploadAsset) calling reigh-data-fetch and timeline-import; raise TimelineVersionConflictError analogue on 409 and retry up to N=3 with re-fetch + re-apply (SD-007). Resolve open contract #3 (asset upload contract) by reading the render-outputs RLS migration and DataProvider.uploadAsset signature; pick a single bucket + signed-URL pattern. PHASE 3 — Worker: artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py exposes a polling loop or HTTP endpoint matching the answer from Phase 0 spike. It accepts the BanodocoTaskPayload envelope, dispatches by intent to existing executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc. via the orchestrator registry), writes back via the SupabaseDataProvider with the supplied correlation_id and expected_version, and emits status transitions queued -> running -> complete/failed via the resolved render-lifecycle channel. Add a `worker` subcommand to artagents/pipeline.py. Service-role key gates only the timeline-import write (SD-009). PHASE 4 — CLI collapse: rewrite artagents/core/project/cli.py to expose `list / show <uuid> / edit <uuid>` plus thin wrappers for add-clip / move-clip / set-theme that compose @banodoco/timeline-ops semantics in Python (port the small ops or call out to a node helper). Delete artagents/core/project/{timeline.py,materialize.py} and the placement symbols in schema.py; keep project.py, source.py, run.py for the local provenance cache (sources/, runs/). Update artagents/core/executor/cli.py so --project accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via SupabaseDataProvider. Rewrite tests/test_project_cli.py + delete or rewrite tests/test_project_materialize.py and test_project_schema.py. PHASE 5 — Sequence support: extend the worker to recognize SequenceDraft-shaped tasks; import the schema from banodoco_timeline_schema (or codegen Python types from the Zod source if missing); validate, then materialize via a small Python applySequenceDraftToTimeline equivalent or an out-of-process node call to the sprint-08 sequences module; fall back to an explicit \"unsupported sequence\" failure when the clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY (open contract #5 resolution). PHASE 6 — Parity tests: add tests/test_renderer_parity.py that exports the sprint-08 fixtures (createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture, createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture) to JSON via a small node helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and asserts hash match against a reigh-app golden. Add tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py, tests/test_banodoco_worker.py, tests/test_sequence_support.py for unit coverage. Update tests/test_open_in_reigh.py and tests/test_project_runs.py for SD-008 baseline_snapshot. Throughout, preserve source_uuid/generation/pool_id/clip_order on every clip the worker writes (SD-003) and never reintroduce a structured ref field. Document each open-contract resolution in the module docstring of the file that owns it so future agents see the why.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform: swap AA's Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, implement a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that talks to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read), build the banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes (correlation_id, expected_version, user_jwt) and writes back via timeline-import, collapse AA's CLI to operate on reigh-app project IDs (no more parallel placement schema), support sequence-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema's SequenceDraft, and verify renderer parity using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve the 5 open contracts (worker reception model, JWT+service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor the 12 settled decisions imported from the doc.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform (v3)

## Overview

Goal unchanged: make AA a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform. v3 addresses four concrete v2 gaps without changing the architecture: (1) re-ground the worker contract on the **actual** existing endpoints (`claim-next-task`, `update-task-status`, `get-task-status`) and the existing orchestrator handler at `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py`; (2) tighten the DataProvider surface to drop methods that don't exist in the canonical TS contract; (3) pin a concrete node-helper runtime (root `package.json` consuming published `@banodoco/*` packages, no sibling-checkout TS imports); (4) keep all v2 settled decisions intact.

Key v3 corrections (with citations):

- **Worker reception (FLAG-006, callers, issue_hints, scope)**: There is no `task-status` edge function. The actual pieces are:
  - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/claim-next-task/index.ts` — accepts `run_type ∈ {gpu, api}` today; does **not** filter on `worker_pool`.
  - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/get-task-status/index.ts` — POST body, returns `{status}` only. This is what `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` actually hits.
  - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/update-task-status/index.ts` — accepts `status / output_location / error / attempts / clear_worker`.
  - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/index.ts` — requires `file_data+filename` or `storage_path` (file/storage oriented; **not** appropriate for timeline writes).
  - `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` — the existing pinned banodoco-worker pattern that polls via `task_utils` and writes via `update_timeline_config_versioned`.

  v3 picks the **direct-Supabase** path that matches the existing banodoco handler: AA's worker uses `task_utils` against `claim-next-task` (with `run_type='banodoco-worker'`, the value `delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts` already enqueues), writes the timeline via `timeline-import`, then calls `update-task-status` with `status='complete'` and a JSON-encoded envelope in `output_location` carrying `{correlation_id, project_id, timeline_id, config_version}`. `complete_task` is **not** used (file/storage shape doesn't fit). `get-task-status` is what reigh-app's poll observes.

- **Cross-repo PRs (Phase 0 step 2)**: Two concrete supporting changes are scoped here:
  - Extend `claim-next-task` to accept `run_type='banodoco-worker'` (so AA can claim, mirroring the existing handler's `run_type` filter), plus a sibling count RPC update.
  - Extend `reigh-data-fetch`'s `TIMELINES_SELECT` to include `config_version` (carried over from v2).

  No changes to `update-task-status` schema — JSON envelope inside `output_location` is the agreed shim per the gate's guidance ("correlation_id can ride in `output_location` as a JSON envelope").

- **DataProvider surface (correctness-1)**: Tighten to match `data/DataProvider.ts` exactly. Required: `loadTimeline`, `saveTimeline`, `loadAssetRegistry`, `resolveAssetUrl`. Optional: `registerAsset`, `uploadAsset`, `loadCheckpoints`, `saveCheckpoint`, `loadWaveform` (no `saveWaveform`), `loadAssetProfile` (no `saveProfile`/`loadProfile`). Drop the made-up writers from success criteria.

- **Node-helper runtime (all_locations)**: Add a root `package.json` at the AA repo root (currently absent — the existing `package.json` is `remotion/package.json`). It declares `@banodoco/timeline-ops` and `@banodoco/timeline-schema` as **published-package** dependencies (no `file:` paths to sibling repos), and the helpers (`ops_helper.mjs`, `sequence_helper.mjs`, `export_fixtures.mjs`) live under `scripts/node/` and import by package name only. No `tsx` or path aliases needed. The Remotion bundle keeps its own `remotion/package.json` independent.

- **All v2 settled decisions stay**: mutator-based `save_timeline` retry, expected_version required, asset upload to `timeline-assets`, sequence via public `sequence.ts` API, baseline_snapshot at `runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot`, three-package version reconcile, fixtures from `testing.ts`, user-JWT for `timeline-import`.

Repository shape that matters (additions in v3 marked):
- `package.json` (**new at repo root**), `scripts/node/{ops_helper.mjs,sequence_helper.mjs,export_fixtures.mjs}` (**new**).
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx`, `remotion/package.json`, `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/{timeline-composition,timeline-schema}/**`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}`.
- `artagents/core/reigh/{__init__.py,env.py,errors.py,supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,data_provider.py,task_client.py,sequences.py}` (**new**).
- `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py` (**new**).
- `artagents/timeline.py`, `artagents/packs/builtin/{reigh_data,open_in_reigh}/run.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`.
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py`, plus new test files.

## Phase 0: Discovery, Cross-Repo Supporting Changes, Tooling

### Step 1: Capture cross-repo contracts and write the integration brief (`docs/integration_contracts.md`)
**Scope:** Small (research + doc)
1. **Read** the canonical reigh-app and orchestrator surfaces, quoting line ranges into `docs/integration_contracts.md`:
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/DataProvider.ts` and `data/AssetResolver.ts` — exact required and optional methods.
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/SupabaseDataProvider.ts` — `timeline-assets` bucket, path layout, `registerAsset` flow.
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/{index.ts,testing.ts,sequence.ts}` — fixture exports live in `testing.ts`; sequence API is `validateSequenceDraft` / `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` / `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/{timeline-import,reigh-data-fetch,claim-next-task,get-task-status,update-task-status,complete_task}/index.ts` — auth gates, request schemas, version semantics.
   - `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` and `task_utils.py` — the existing banodoco worker pattern AA mirrors.
   - `../reigh-app/.../delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts` — envelope shape (`task_type='banodoco_timeline_generate'`, `run_type='banodoco-worker'`, `worker_pool='banodoco'`, optional `current_timeline`, `correlation_id`, `expected_version`, `user_jwt`).
2. **Document** in `docs/integration_contracts.md` the resolutions for the five open contracts:
   - **#1 Worker reception** — AA polls `claim-next-task` with `run_type='banodoco-worker'` (mirrors the existing orchestrator handler). After processing, AA calls `update-task-status` with `status='complete' | 'failed'` and a JSON-encoded envelope in `output_location` containing `{correlation_id, project_id, timeline_id, config_version}`. `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reads `get-task-status` (POST body, returns `{status}`).
   - **#2 JWT/service-role lifecycle** — claim response delivers a short-lived user JWT bound to the project owner (Phase 0 step 2 PR ensures this); AA's terminal `timeline-import` write uses that JWT. Service-role key is used only for `claim-next-task`/`update-task-status` (the orchestrator-internal endpoints), never against `timeline-import`.
   - **#3 Asset upload** — `timeline-assets` bucket, path `${userId}/${timelineId}/${Date.now()}-${filename}`, then `registerAsset`.
   - **#4 Render lifecycle** — `update-task-status` flips the row that `get-task-status` reads. No Realtime channel for v1. `complete_task` is **not** used (it requires `file_data`/`storage_path`).
   - **#5 Sequence schema** — public TS API in `sequence.ts`; AA shells to a node helper.
3. **Reconcile** the three shared packages (`@banodoco/timeline-composition`, `@banodoco/timeline-schema`, `@banodoco/timeline-ops`). Pin matching versions in `remotion/package.json` (for the Remotion bundle) and the **new** root `package.json` (for the node helpers, see Step 3). Strategy is recorded in `docs/integration_contracts.md`.

### Step 2: Reigh-app + orchestrator supporting PRs
**Scope:** Small (cross-repo)
1. **Extend** `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/claim-next-task/index.ts` to accept `run_type='banodoco-worker'` (current schema is `'gpu' | 'api'` only) and the corresponding count RPC. Without this, AA can't claim banodoco tasks via the canonical endpoint. The orchestrator handler at `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` likely already filters by this `run_type` — confirm and align.
2. **Extend** `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` `TIMELINES_SELECT` to include `config_version`. (Carried from v2.)
3. **Confirm** the claim response surfaces a short-lived user JWT bound to the project owner. If not, add that field to the same PR — Phase 3 depends on it.
4. **Open** the PR(s); track merge state in `docs/integration_contracts.md`. AA's Phase 3 work is gated on these merging.

### Step 3: Add a root Node toolchain for helpers (`package.json`, `scripts/node/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `package.json` at the AA repo root with:
   - `"private": true`
   - `dependencies`: `@banodoco/timeline-ops`, `@banodoco/timeline-schema` (matching versions per Step 1 reconcile). **No `file:` paths to sibling repos** — only published packages so the toolchain doesn't depend on a particular checkout layout.
   - `scripts`: `"ops": "node scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs"`, `"sequence": "node scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs"`, `"fixtures": "node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs"`.
2. **Create** `scripts/node/` (empty for now; populated by Phase 4 / Phase 5 / Phase 6). Helpers import via package names only (e.g. `import { createTimelineCommandRunner } from '@banodoco/timeline-ops';`). No TS path aliases, no `tsx`. Pure ESM `.mjs`.
3. **Run** `npm install` at the repo root; commit `package-lock.json`.

## Phase 1: Renderer Swap

### Step 4: Replace hand-rolled Remotion comp with `<TimelineComposition>` (`remotion/src/Root.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `remotion/src/Root.tsx` to a thin `<Composition>` wrapper that imports `TimelineComposition` and `getTimelineDurationInFrames` from `@banodoco/timeline-composition`. Forward `defaultProps`/`calculateMetadata`. Delete local `RenderClip`, `AdosCard`, `AdosLowerThird`, `CornerLogo`, and local `TimelineClip`/`TimelineConfig`/`AssetRegistry` types.
2. **Pull** the existing `defaultProps` JSON shape forward unchanged. `calculateMetadata` derives `width`/`height`/`fps`/`durationInFrames` via `getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)`.
3. **Verify** with `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` (or `node remotion/__smoke__/bundle.mjs`); render `examples/hype.timeline.full.json` headless.

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + DataProvider Surface (Tightened)

### Step 5: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move `_read_env_value`, `_candidate_env_files`, `_env_first`, `resolve_api_url`, `resolve_pat` from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105`.
2. **Add** `resolve_timeline_import_url(...)`, `resolve_claim_url(...)` (for `claim-next-task`), `resolve_task_status_update_url(...)` (for `update-task-status`), `resolve_task_status_get_url(...)` (for `get-task-status`), and `resolve_service_role_key(...)` (used only for the claim/status endpoints, never for `timeline-import`).
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export from `artagents.core.reigh.env` so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing.

### Step 6: Implement `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the canonical TS surface exactly (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,errors.py,data_provider.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError` and `TimelineNotFoundError` (mapped from HTTP 409 / 404).
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin `urllib.request` POST wrapper. Auth selector parameterised: `auth=("user_jwt", token)` or `auth=("service_role", key)`. Maps 409 → `TimelineVersionConflictError`, 404 → `TimelineNotFoundError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_io.py` with the mutator-based save:

   ```python
   def save_timeline(
       *,
       project_id: str,
       timeline_id: str,
       mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig],
       auth: Auth,
       retries: int = 3,
       force: bool = False,
   ) -> SaveResult: ...
   ```

   Loop: load `(timeline, version)`, apply mutator, POST with `expected_version=version` (or omit only when `force=True`). On 409, re-fetch + re-apply, up to `retries`. Reject `expected_version=None` on the normal path; only `force=True` bypasses, and it logs.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` whose method surface **exactly** matches `data/DataProvider.ts` (and its inherited `AssetResolver`):
   - **Required**: `load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> tuple[TimelineConfig, int]`, `save_timeline(project_id, timeline_id, mutator, auth, retries=3, force=False)`, `load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id) -> AssetRegistry`, `resolve_asset_url(asset_entry) -> str`.
   - **Optional**: `register_asset(project_id, timeline_id, asset_entry)`, `upload_asset(local_path_or_bytes, *, content_type, user_id, timeline_id, filename) -> AssetEntry` (uploads to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then `register_asset`), `load_checkpoints(project_id, timeline_id)`, `save_checkpoint(project_id, timeline_id, checkpoint)`, `load_waveform(asset_id)`, `load_asset_profile(asset_id)`.
   - **Explicitly NOT** present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile` — these are not on the canonical TS surface.
5. **Round-trip** every fetched timeline through `artagents.timeline.validate_timeline` / `load_timeline` / `save_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.

### Step 7: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy path with `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., mutator=lambda t, v: merged_timeline)`. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` so the default path mocks the DataProvider; keep escape-hatch test cases.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker (Direct-Supabase Pattern)

### Step 8: Implement task-protocol client (`artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py` wrapping the **actual** orchestrator endpoints — no fictional functions:
   - `claim_next_task(run_type='banodoco-worker', auth=("service_role", key)) -> ClaimResult | None` — POST to `claim-next-task`. Returns the envelope `{ task_id, run_id, project_id, params, user_jwt, ... }` or `None` if the queue is empty.
   - `update_task_status(task_id, *, status, output_location=None, error=None, auth=("service_role", key))` — POST to `update-task-status`. `output_location` accepts a JSON-stringified envelope `{correlation_id, project_id, timeline_id, config_version}` since the schema has no first-class fields for these.
   - `get_task_status(task_id, auth=...) -> {status: str}` — POST to `get-task-status`. Used by tests; reigh-app's `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` is the production caller.
2. **Document** in the module docstring that `complete_task` is **not** used (file/storage-only shape; doesn't fit timeline writes).

### Step 9: Implement worker (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. Module docstring records the resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with file:line citations to `claim-next-task/index.ts`, `update-task-status/index.ts`, `get-task-status/index.ts`, and `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py`.
2. **Reception**: long-running poll loop calling `task_client.claim_next_task(run_type='banodoco-worker', auth=service_role)`. Service-role auth is required by `claim-next-task`; that's the orchestrator-internal channel and is **not** the same as `timeline-import` auth.
3. **Snapshot fast-path**: if `params.current_timeline` is present, dispatch using it directly (no extra `load_timeline`). Otherwise call `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id)`.
4. **Dispatch by `intent`** to existing AA executors via the orchestrator registry under `artagents/core/orchestrator/`. Each executor returns a mutator callable.
5. **Carry-fields**: every emitted clip populates `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003).
6. **Local provenance**: write `runs/<run_id>.json` with `metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload))` (SD-008).
7. **Timeline write**: `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(project_id, timeline_id, mutator, auth=("user_jwt", task.user_jwt), retries=3)`. `expected_version=params.expected_version` is required; `force=True` is forbidden.
8. **Status report**: on success, `task_client.update_task_status(task_id, status='complete', output_location=json.dumps({correlation_id, project_id, timeline_id, config_version: result.new_version}), auth=service_role)`. On failure, `status='failed'` with `error=str(exc)`. `complete_task` is not called.
9. **Wire** `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help`. The `--pool` arg is informational; the actual filter is `run_type='banodoco-worker'` on the claim endpoint.

## Phase 4: CLI Collapse — Operate on reigh-app Project UUIDs

### Step 10: Delete the placement schema (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,project.py}`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Delete** `artagents/core/project/timeline.py` and `artagents/core/project/materialize.py`.
2. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/schema.py`: remove placement symbols (`TIMELINE_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `build_project_timeline`, `build_placement`, `validate_project_timeline`, `validate_placement`, `validate_reference`, `REF_KINDS`, `source_ref`, `run_ref`). Keep project/source/run schema constants.
3. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/project.py`: stop writing `timeline.json` from `create_project`; delete `load_project_timeline`/`save_project_timeline`. `show_project` either fetches via `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline` (if `project.json#project_id` is set) or reports cache-only state.
4. **Keep** `source.py`, `run.py`, `paths.py`, `jsonio.py`. Add an opaque `project_id` field on `project.json`.

### Step 11: Rewrite the `projects` CLI surface using a node `timeline-ops` helper (`artagents/core/project/cli.py`, `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `artagents/core/project/cli.py` so subcommands are: `create`, `show`, `source add`, `list <project_id>`, `edit <project_id>`. Drop placement verbs.
2. **Create** `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` (Node ESM) that imports `createTimelineCommandRunner` from `@banodoco/timeline-ops` (root `package.json` dependency, Phase 0 step 3). It reads `{timeline, version, commands}` JSON from stdin, applies the commands via `createTimelineCommandRunner`, and writes the resulting timeline JSON to stdout. Errors go to stderr and exit non-zero.
3. **Wire** edit verbs (`add-clip`, `move-clip`, `set-theme`) so they build a command list, shell to `node scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs`, and use the resulting timeline as `mutator`'s return value inside `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., retries=3)`. `expected_version` is required.
4. **Update** `artagents/core/executor/cli.py:_cmd_run` so `--project` accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via `SupabaseDataProvider`. Keep the slug path as cache-only/offline mode.

### Step 12: Rewrite project-CLI tests (`tests/test_project_cli.py`, `tests/test_project_materialize.py`, `tests/test_project_schema.py`, `tests/test_project_runs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_*` against mocked `reigh-data-fetch` + `timeline-import` (force a 409 and assert the mutator is re-applied; assert exhaustion at N=3; assert that omitting `expected_version` raises rather than force-saving).
2. **Delete** `tests/test_project_materialize.py` (placement-only).
3. **Trim** `tests/test_project_schema.py` to project/source/run schema only.
4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to assert `metadata.baseline_snapshot` is a sha256 hex string at exactly that path.

## Phase 5: Sequence Support via Public `sequence.ts` API

### Step 13: Sequence helper + worker wiring (`scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`, `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs` — Node ESM that imports `validateSequenceDraft`, `applySequenceDraftToTimeline`, and `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` from `@banodoco/timeline-schema` (root `package.json` dependency). Reads `{timeline, version, sequenceDraft}` from stdin; emits validated/applied timeline JSON to stdout, or an error envelope.
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py` that shells to the helper and returns a mutator usable by `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline`. If the helper reports `clipType` not in `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES`, raise `UnsupportedSequenceError`.
3. **Detect** sequence-shaped intents in the worker: when `params.intent` carries a `SequenceDraft`, run `sequences.validate(...)`. If unsupported, complete the task as `failed` with `error=f"unsupported sequence: {clipType}"`.

## Phase 6: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 14: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert (a) `load_timeline` returns `(config, config_version)` and round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `save_timeline` re-applies the mutator on 409 and exhausts at N=3, (c) `save_timeline` raises when `expected_version` is omitted unless `force=True`, (d) `timeline-import` only ever sees user-JWT, (e) `upload_asset` writes to `timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then calls `register_asset`, (f) the DataProvider surface is exactly the canonical set (presence test for required + optional, absence test for `save_waveform`/`save_profile`/`load_profile`).
2. **Add** `tests/test_task_client.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert `claim_next_task` POSTs `run_type='banodoco-worker'` to `claim-next-task` and returns the envelope; `update_task_status` POSTs to `update-task-status` with `output_location` carrying the JSON envelope; `get_task_status` POSTs to `get-task-status`; `complete_task` is **not** called from anywhere in AA.
3. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake claim response. Assert intent dispatch, `correlation_id` echo (in the `output_location` envelope), `expected_version` propagation, snapshot fast-path with and without `current_timeline`, `update-task-status` call shape on success and failure, carry-fields on emitted clips, `metadata.baseline_snapshot` location.
4. **Add** `tests/test_sequence_support.py`: a `SequenceDraft` with a `clipType` in `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` materialises through the helper; an unrecognised `clipType` surfaces `unsupported sequence` and the worker reports `status='failed'`.

### Step 15: Renderer parity smoke importing fixtures from `testing.ts` (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs` — Node ESM that imports `createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture` and `createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture` from the **published** `@banodoco/timeline-schema/testing` (or whichever package subpath re-exports `testing.ts`). Not from `index.ts`. Not from a sibling-checkout TS file.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells to `node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, compares hash to `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/`. Skip with a documented reason if the helper or goldens are unavailable.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** — contract resolution, cross-repo PRs (claim-next-task `banodoco-worker` + reigh-data-fetch `config_version`), root `package.json` for node helpers.
2. **Phase 1** — renderer swap (isolated; unblocks parity tests).
3. **Phase 2** — DataProvider with the **canonical** method surface, mutator-based retry, asset upload.
4. **Phase 3** — task-protocol client + worker (gated on Phase 0 PRs landing).
5. **Phase 4** — CLI collapse + node ops helper.
6. **Phase 5** — sequence support via node helper.
7. **Phase 6** — parity + new unit tests.

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_task_client.py tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end `claim-next-task → execute → timeline-import → update-task-status → get-task-status` flow against staging reigh-app.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py`.
5. After Phase 5: `pytest tests/test_sequence_support.py`.
6. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected

        Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform across six phases: (1) swap AA's hand-rolled Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, (2) build a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that POSTs to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read) with expected_version retry, (3) build a banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes and writes back via timeline-import, (4) collapse AA's parallel placement schema/CLI so verbs operate on reigh-app project IDs, (5) support SequenceDraft-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema, (6) add renderer parity tests using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture.         ### Key Evidence
        - AA's renderer is a 496-line hand-rolled <TimelineComposition> in remotion/src/Root.tsx that ignores the already-installed @banodoco/timeline-composition v0.0.5 package. All of this becomes either local-cache-only (sources/, runs/ retained for provenance) or is deleted; the new CLI must operate on reigh-app project UUIDs via reigh-data-fetch + timeline-import + @banodoco/timeline-ops.         ### Constraints
        - Do not introduce a new source: SourceRef|RunRef field on TimelineClip — SD-003 explicitly rejects this. Use the existing source_uuid + generation carry-fields instead.
- Do not reintroduce or extend the parallel placement schema (artagents/core/project/schema.py:build_placement and friends). SD-006 collapses it; sources/ and runs/ directories survive only as local provenance cache.
- Do not import from reigh-app internals. SD-010 mandates building against the public SDK at src/tools/video-editor/index.ts (sprint-08, @publicContract). DataProvider, TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError, fixture constructors come from there.
- Do not put the service-role key on the user-JWT path. SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the AA worker's final timeline-import write. Mirrors reigh-app's SD-022.
- Do not break tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py — byte-equivalent round-trip and allowlist parity with @banodoco/timeline-schema are gating invariants.
- Do not author one-way (AA composes only). SD-012 requires two-way authoring: both reigh-app and AA must be able to write.
- Do not skip the @banodoco/timeline-composition version reconcile. AA's v0.0.5 vs reigh-app sprint-08's local-path v0.0.2 must converge on a shared tag before parity tests are meaningful — flagged as a Phase 1 blocker by the doc.
- Do not silently bypass expected_version. SD-007: timeline-import push retries on conflict with cap N=3, surfaces failure to caller on exhaustion.
- Do not delete sources/ and runs/ directories during the placement-schema collapse. Per SD-006 they remain as local provenance cache; only the placement schema and local timeline.json-as-source-of-truth go.
- Do not lose AA-generated provenance on round-trip. New clips emitted via the worker must populate source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order so reigh-app's TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS allowlist preserves them. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 to AA's local run record for SD-008 drift detection.
- Do not invoke megaplan, the megaplan skill, or nested planning loops — explicit per harness directive.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Honour the SD-011 phase order, but front-load two discovery spikes before code so the worker design isn't rewritten twice. PHASE 0 (~half day, before Phase 1): freeze the @banodoco/timeline-composition version (cut a v0.0.6 tag both AA and reigh-app sprint-08 consume, OR temporarily switch AA to a workspace file:.. path matching reigh-app); resolve open contracts #1 (worker reception model — HTTP push vs poll-queue and ack envelope) and #4 (render lifecycle / realtime channel) by reading delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300 and pollBanodocoTaskStatus, document the answers in a top-of-module docstring on the new worker file. PHASE 1 — Renderer swap: rewrite remotion/src/Root.tsx to a ~30-line file: <Composition id=\"TimelineComposition\" component={TimelineComposition} defaultProps={...} calculateMetadata={({props}) => ({width, height, fps, durationInFrames: getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)})} />, importing from @banodoco/timeline-composition. Delete the AdosCard/AdosLowerThird/CornerLogo helpers — those become theme-package effects. Run remotion smoke; render a hype fixture; compare hash to a reigh-app golden if available, otherwise visual diff. PHASE 2 — Supabase client: add artagents/core/reigh/{env.py,supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py}. env.py extracts _candidate_env_files/_env_first from packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py into a shared helper. supabase_client.py wraps urllib.request POSTs with auth header selection (user JWT vs service-role per call site). data_provider.py implements a Python mirror of the sprint-08 DataProvider interface (fetch, persist, uploadAsset) calling reigh-data-fetch and timeline-import; raise TimelineVersionConflictError analogue on 409 and retry up to N=3 with re-fetch + re-apply (SD-007). Resolve open contract #3 (asset upload contract) by reading the render-outputs RLS migration and DataProvider.uploadAsset signature; pick a single bucket + signed-URL pattern. PHASE 3 — Worker: artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py exposes a polling loop or HTTP endpoint matching the answer from Phase 0 spike. It accepts the BanodocoTaskPayload envelope, dispatches by intent to existing executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc. via the orchestrator registry), writes back via the SupabaseDataProvider with the supplied correlation_id and expected_version, and emits status transitions queued -> running -> complete/failed via the resolved render-lifecycle channel. Add a `worker` subcommand to artagents/pipeline.py. Service-role key gates only the timeline-import write (SD-009). PHASE 4 — CLI collapse: rewrite artagents/core/project/cli.py to expose `list / show <uuid> / edit <uuid>` plus thin wrappers for add-clip / move-clip / set-theme that compose @banodoco/timeline-ops semantics in Python (port the small ops or call out to a node helper). Delete artagents/core/project/{timeline.py,materialize.py} and the placement symbols in schema.py; keep project.py, source.py, run.py for the local provenance cache (sources/, runs/). Update artagents/core/executor/cli.py so --project accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via SupabaseDataProvider. Rewrite tests/test_project_cli.py + delete or rewrite tests/test_project_materialize.py and test_project_schema.py. PHASE 5 — Sequence support: extend the worker to recognize SequenceDraft-shaped tasks; import the schema from banodoco_timeline_schema (or codegen Python types from the Zod source if missing); validate, then materialize via a small Python applySequenceDraftToTimeline equivalent or an out-of-process node call to the sprint-08 sequences module; fall back to an explicit \"unsupported sequence\" failure when the clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY (open contract #5 resolution). PHASE 6 — Parity tests: add tests/test_renderer_parity.py that exports the sprint-08 fixtures (createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture, createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture) to JSON via a small node helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and asserts hash match against a reigh-app golden. Add tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py, tests/test_banodoco_worker.py, tests/test_sequence_support.py for unit coverage. Update tests/test_open_in_reigh.py and tests/test_project_runs.py for SD-008 baseline_snapshot. Throughout, preserve source_uuid/generation/pool_id/clip_order on every clip the worker writes (SD-003) and never reintroduce a structured ref field. Document each open-contract resolution in the module docstring of the file that owns it so future agents see the why.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform: swap AA's Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, implement a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that talks to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read), build the banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes (correlation_id, expected_version, user_jwt) and writes back via timeline-import, collapse AA's CLI to operate on reigh-app project IDs (no more parallel placement schema), support sequence-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema's SequenceDraft, and verify renderer parity using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve the 5 open contracts (worker reception model, JWT+service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor the 12 settled decisions imported from the doc.

User notes and answers:
- CONTRACT RESOLUTION (post-cycle-3 escalation): The 4 cross-repo blockers from gate v3 have been resolved as follows. Plan revision should incorporate these as settled facts. (1) WORKER CLAIM PROTOCOL: Do NOT extend supabase/functions/claim-next-task. The orchestrator at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py ALREADY implements the banodoco worker-pool (BANODOCO_POOL constant, banodoco_timeline_generate task type, SD-034 envelope validation, JWKS-verified user_jwt, update_timeline_config_versioned write path, is_worker_pool_available check with BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC default 30s). AA implements the WORKER side of this pool — Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims banodoco_timeline_generate tasks, verifies user_jwt against Reigh's Supabase JWKS, executes, then writes via update_timeline_config_versioned (the versioned RPC). The reference Node+Python worker image is at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/ — AA can use that as the integration target. (2) TASK-STATUS ENDPOINT: The reigh-app sprint-8 branch (sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator) has been patched locally (commit ee2e6f10c, not yet pushed) to add a NEW supabase/functions/task-status/ edge function: GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>, JWT-authed via _shared/auth.ts, returns {status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}} sourced from tasks.result_data JSON column. This is exactly what pollBanodocoTaskStatus expects. AA does NOT need to call this endpoint itself — reigh-app's poller calls it. AA just writes result_data via update-task-status. (3) UPDATE-TASK-STATUS RESULT FIELD: update-task-status now accepts an optional result_data: Record<string, unknown> in its request body, persisted to tasks.result_data. AA's worker, on completion, calls update-task-status with status='Complete' and result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} so the new task-status reader can surface those fields. result_data column already existed in tasks (migration 20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql, jsonb default '{}'). (4) STATUS ENUM CASING: AA adapts to Title Case. Use Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled — these are the only values update-task-status accepts. Do NOT use lowercase. (5) PACKAGE DEPS: @banodoco/timeline-ops and @banodoco/timeline-schema are private and unpublished. For AA's helper Node toolchain (Phase 1 fixture export, Phase 4 ops helpers), use file: dependencies in a root package.json pointing at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript. Helpers that need APIs not exported by the local packages (e.g., createTimelineCommandRunner from reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/commands/index.ts) should be replaced by direct timeline-ops calls (addClip/moveClip/setTimelineTheme/etc) since those primitives ARE exported. PHASE 0 of execute should add this root package.json and verify the file: imports work. (6) DATAPROVIDER METHOD LIST: Tighten Step 5's DataProvider Python interface to mirror the actual TypeScript contract: load_timeline, save_timeline, load_asset_registry, resolve_asset_url, optional register_asset, optional upload_asset, optional load_checkpoints/save_checkpoint, optional load_waveform (no save_waveform), optional load_asset_profile (no load_profile/save_profile). Remove fictional save_waveform/save_profile/load_profile from success criteria.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform (v4)

## Overview

Goal unchanged: make AA a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform — renderer delegation to `<TimelineComposition>`, a Python `SupabaseDataProvider`, a `banodoco`-pool worker, CLI collapse, sequence-draft support, renderer parity tests.

v4 incorporates the post-cycle-3 contract resolutions provided in user notes (treated as settled facts):

- **Worker claim (resolution #1)**: Do **not** extend `supabase/functions/claim-next-task`. The orchestrator at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` already implements the banodoco pool (`BANODOCO_POOL`, `banodoco_timeline_generate` task type, SD-034 envelope validation, JWKS-verified `user_jwt`, `update_timeline_config_versioned` write path, `is_worker_pool_available` with `BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC` default 30s). AA implements the **worker side** of this pool — a Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims `banodoco_timeline_generate` tasks, JWKS-verifies the user_jwt, executes, then writes via `update_timeline_config_versioned` (the versioned Postgres RPC, not the timeline-import edge function). Reference Node+Python worker image at `banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/` is the integration target.
- **task-status endpoint (resolution #2)**: A new `supabase/functions/task-status/` edge function already exists on the reigh-app sprint-8 branch (commit `ee2e6f10c`, not yet pushed). It is a `GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>`, JWT-authed, returning `{status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}}` sourced from `tasks.result_data`. AA does **not** call it — reigh-app's poller does. AA only needs to write `result_data` so the poller sees what it expects.
- **update-task-status result_data (resolution #3)**: `update-task-status` accepts optional `result_data: Record<string, unknown>` (persisted to `tasks.result_data`; column existed since migration `20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql`, jsonb default `'{}'`). On completion AA calls `update-task-status` with `status='Complete'` and `result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id}`. The new task-status reader surfaces those fields. The v3 "JSON envelope inside `output_location`" shim is replaced by this first-class field.
- **Status enum casing (resolution #4)**: Use **Title Case** — `Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled`. Lowercase is rejected.
- **Package deps (resolution #5)**: `@banodoco/timeline-ops` and `@banodoco/timeline-schema` are private and unpublished. AA's root `package.json` uses `file:` dependencies pointing at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript`. This is the only deployment target for now (sibling-checkout dev environment); npm publish is out of scope. APIs not exported by the local packages (`createTimelineCommandRunner` lives in `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/commands/index.ts`, not in `timeline-ops`) are replaced by **direct timeline-ops primitives** that ARE exported (`addClip`, `moveClip`, `setTimelineTheme`, etc.). Sequence and fixture imports also follow whatever the local packages export — Phase 0 records the exact symbols and adjusts the helpers.
- **DataProvider surface (resolution #6)**: Required `load_timeline`, `save_timeline`, `load_asset_registry`, `resolve_asset_url`. Optional `register_asset`, `upload_asset`, `load_checkpoints`/`save_checkpoint`, `load_waveform` (no `save_waveform`), `load_asset_profile` (no `load_profile`/`save_profile`). Carried over from v3.

All v3 settled decisions still hold (mutator-based retry, expected_version required, asset upload to `timeline-assets`, baseline_snapshot at `runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot`, two-way authoring, no service-role key against the timeline write path). Two of those are now mechanically different:

- **Timeline write path**: AA writes via the `update_timeline_config_versioned` Postgres RPC (the versioned write the orchestrator handler already uses), not the `timeline-import` edge function. The mutator-based retry (`save_timeline(mutator, retries=3)`) wraps the RPC; on a version mismatch the RPC returns a discriminated result that AA maps to `TimelineVersionConflictError` and retries up to N=3.
- **Auth**: `update_timeline_config_versioned` is called with the JWKS-verified user_jwt from the claim envelope. Service-role is used only against the orchestrator's task endpoints (claim/update-status), never against the timeline-config RPC.

Repository shape that matters (additions in v4 marked):
- `package.json` (**new at repo root**, `file:` deps), `scripts/node/{ops_helper.mjs,sequence_helper.mjs,export_fixtures.mjs}` (**new**).
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx`, `remotion/package.json`, `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/{timeline-composition,timeline-schema}/**`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}`.
- `artagents/core/reigh/{__init__.py,env.py,errors.py,supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,data_provider.py,task_client.py,sequences.py,jwks.py}` (**new** — `jwks.py` is added for SD-009 user_jwt verification).
- `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py` (**new**).
- `artagents/timeline.py`, `artagents/packs/builtin/{reigh_data,open_in_reigh}/run.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`.
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py`, plus new test files.

## Phase 0: Discovery, Tooling, No Cross-Repo PRs Required

### Step 1: Capture cross-repo contracts and write the integration brief (`docs/integration_contracts.md`)
**Scope:** Small (research + doc)
1. **Read** and quote line ranges into `docs/integration_contracts.md` (new):
   - `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` — `BANODOCO_POOL`, `banodoco_timeline_generate`, SD-034 envelope, `update_timeline_config_versioned`, `is_worker_pool_available`, `BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC`.
   - `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/task_utils.py` — claim/update primitives AA mirrors.
   - `../banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/` — reference Node+Python worker image (the integration target shape).
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/task-status/index.ts` (commit `ee2e6f10c`, sprint-8 branch) — GET response shape: `{status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}}`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/update-task-status/{index.ts,types.ts,request.ts}` — confirms `result_data: Record<string, unknown>` field, status enum `Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` — `TIMELINES_SELECT` columns; if `config_version` is missing, that single small change is the only cross-repo PR scope (see Step 2).
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/{DataProvider.ts,SupabaseDataProvider.ts,AssetResolver.ts}` — canonical method surface, `timeline-assets` bucket layout.
   - `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops/typescript/src/index.ts` — exports `addClip`, `moveClip`, `setTimelineTheme`, etc. (no `createTimelineCommandRunner`).
   - `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema/typescript/src/index.ts` — exports `schemas`, `resolveTheme`, etc. (Phase 5 records the actual sequence/testing exports).
2. **Document** in `docs/integration_contracts.md` the resolutions for the five open contracts (now grounded in the resolution notes):
   - **#1 Worker reception** — AA mirrors the existing banodoco-worker pattern (poll the orchestrator's claim endpoint with the same shape `task_utils` uses; the orchestrator already filters for `BANODOCO_POOL`).
   - **#2 JWT/service-role** — claim envelope carries a JWKS-verifiable user_jwt; AA verifies via Reigh's Supabase JWKS, uses that JWT against `update_timeline_config_versioned`. Service-role only on the orchestrator-internal task endpoints.
   - **#3 Asset upload** — `timeline-assets` bucket, `${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}`, then `register_asset`.
   - **#4 Render lifecycle** — AA writes `result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id}` via `update-task-status` with status `Complete`. The pre-existing `task-status` GET edge function (commit `ee2e6f10c`) surfaces `result_data` fields to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
   - **#5 Sequence schema** — record the exact symbols available on the local `@banodoco/timeline-schema` package; if the trusted-sequence/applySequenceDraft APIs are not yet exported, Phase 5 falls back to validating against `banodoco_timeline_schema`'s JSON Schema and emits clips through ordinary `timeline-ops` primitives. No invented imports.
3. **Reconcile** the three shared packages. Pin matching versions in `remotion/package.json` and the new root `package.json`. Strategy is recorded in the doc.

**Scope:** Small

Otherwise skip — Phase 0 step 1 confirms whether this is needed. No claim-next-task or update-task-status changes are required (resolutions #1–#3 confirm the surfaces already exist).

**Scope:** Small

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + DataProvider Surface (Tightened)

### Step 5: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move env helpers (`_read_env_value`, `_candidate_env_files`, `_env_first`, `resolve_api_url`, `resolve_pat`) from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105`.
2. **Add** `resolve_orchestrator_url(...)`, `resolve_supabase_url(...)`, `resolve_service_role_key(...)`, `resolve_jwks_url(...)`. The `update-task-status` URL derives from the orchestrator/Supabase URL.
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export from `artagents.core.reigh.env` so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing.

### Step 6: Implement `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the canonical TS surface exactly (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,errors.py,data_provider.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError`, `TimelineNotFoundError`, `UnsupportedSequenceError`.
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin POST/RPC wrapper. Auth selector parameterised: `auth=("user_jwt", token)` (default) or `auth=("service_role", key)`. `update_timeline_config_versioned` is called as a Supabase RPC; a version-mismatch result maps to `TimelineVersionConflictError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_io.py` with the mutator-based save:

   ```python
   def save_timeline(
       *,
       project_id: str,
       timeline_id: str,
       mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig],
       auth: Auth,
       retries: int = 3,
       force: bool = False,
   ) -> SaveResult: ...
   ```

   Loop: load `(timeline, config_version)` via `reigh-data-fetch`, apply mutator, call `update_timeline_config_versioned(project_id, timeline_id, new_config, expected_version=config_version)`. On version-mismatch result, re-fetch + re-apply, up to `retries`. Reject `expected_version=None` on the normal path; only `force=True` bypasses, and it logs.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` whose method surface matches `data/DataProvider.ts` exactly:
   - **Required**: `load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> tuple[TimelineConfig, int]`, `save_timeline(...)` (wraps `timeline_io.save_timeline`), `load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id) -> AssetRegistry`, `resolve_asset_url(asset_entry) -> str`.
   - **Optional**: `register_asset(...)`, `upload_asset(local_path_or_bytes, *, content_type, user_id, timeline_id, filename) -> AssetEntry` (uploads to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}`, then `register_asset`), `load_checkpoints(...)`, `save_checkpoint(...)`, `load_waveform(asset_id)`, `load_asset_profile(asset_id)`.
   - **Explicitly NOT** present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile`.
5. **Round-trip** every fetched timeline through `artagents.timeline.validate_timeline` / `load_timeline` / `save_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.

### Step 7: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy path with `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., mutator=lambda t, v: merged_timeline)`. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` to mock the DataProvider on the default path.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker (Mirrors banodoco-worker reference image)

### Step 8: Implement task-protocol client and JWKS verifier (`artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/jwks.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py` wrapping the **actual** task endpoints used by the existing orchestrator handler:
   - `claim_next_task(auth=("service_role", key)) -> ClaimResult | None` — POSTs to the orchestrator's claim endpoint with the same shape `task_utils` uses, filtered to `task_type='banodoco_timeline_generate'`. Returns the SD-034 envelope (`{ task_id, run_id, project_id, params, user_jwt, ... }`) or `None` if empty.
   - `update_task_status(task_id, *, status, result_data=None, error=None, auth=("service_role", key))` — POSTs to `update-task-status` with `status ∈ {Queued, In Progress, Complete, Failed, Cancelled}` (Title Case) and optional `result_data: dict` (persisted into `tasks.result_data`).
2. **Document** in the module docstring that AA never calls `complete_task` (file/storage shape) and never calls `task-status` (that's reigh-app's `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reader).
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/jwks.py` — verifies a user_jwt against the JWKS published at `resolve_jwks_url()`. Used to gate the worker's terminal write.

### Step 9: Implement worker (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. Module docstring records the resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with file:line citations to `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` and the local `task-status/index.ts` (commit `ee2e6f10c`).
2. **Reception**: long-running poll loop calling `task_client.claim_next_task(auth=service_role)`. The orchestrator already filters by `BANODOCO_POOL` / `task_type='banodoco_timeline_generate'`; AA defensively rejects any envelope whose `task_type` is not `banodoco_timeline_generate`.
3. **JWKS verification**: before any timeline write, verify `task.user_jwt` via `jwks.verify(task.user_jwt)`. Reject with `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt')` and skip on failure.
4. **Snapshot fast-path**: if `params.current_timeline` is present, dispatch using it directly (skip `load_timeline`). Otherwise call `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id)`.
5. **Dispatch by `intent`** to existing AA executors. Each executor returns a mutator callable.
6. **Carry-fields**: every emitted clip populates `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003).
7. **Local provenance**: write `runs/<run_id>.json` with `metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload))` (SD-008).
8. **Timeline write**: `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(project_id, timeline_id, mutator, auth=("user_jwt", task.user_jwt), retries=3)`. Internally this calls the `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC. `expected_version=params.expected_version` is required; `force=True` is forbidden.
9. **Status report on success**: `task_client.update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': timeline_id}, auth=service_role)`. The new `task-status` reader surfaces these fields to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
10. **Status report on failure**: `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id})`. Title Case, never lowercase.
11. **Wire** `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help`.

The helper reads `{timeline, version, op, args}` JSON from stdin, dispatches by `op` to the right primitive, applies it, and writes the resulting timeline to stdout. **No** `createTimelineCommandRunner` (it doesn't exist in the local package).

**Scope:** Medium

4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to assert `metadata.baseline_snapshot` is a sha256 hex string at exactly that path.

## Phase 5: Sequence Support (against actual local-package exports)

### Step 13: Sequence helper + worker wiring (`scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`, `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Inspect** `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema/typescript/src/` (Phase 0 step 1 records what's actually there). Two cases:
   - **If** `validateSequenceDraft` / `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` / `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` are exported, `scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs` imports and uses them.
   - **If not**, the helper validates the SequenceDraft against the local JSON Schema (`schemas` export) and emits the resulting clips through ordinary `timeline-ops` primitives (`addClip` per clip in the draft). Phase 0 records this fallback decision.
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py` that shells to the helper and returns a mutator usable by `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline`. If the helper reports an unsupported `clipType`, raise `UnsupportedSequenceError`.
3. **Detect** sequence-shaped intents in the worker: when `params.intent` carries a `SequenceDraft`, run `sequences.validate(...)`. On `UnsupportedSequenceError`, complete the task with `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error=f'unsupported sequence: {clipType}')`.

## Phase 6: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 14: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock the Supabase client. Assert (a) `load_timeline` returns `(config, config_version)` and round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `save_timeline` re-applies the mutator on RPC version-mismatch and exhausts at N=3, (c) `save_timeline` raises when `expected_version` is omitted unless `force=True`, (d) `update_timeline_config_versioned` only ever sees `auth=("user_jwt", ...)` (never service-role), (e) `upload_asset` writes to `timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then calls `register_asset`, (f) the DataProvider surface is exactly the canonical set (presence + absence tests).
2. **Add** `tests/test_task_client.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert `claim_next_task` POSTs the orchestrator claim envelope; `update_task_status` POSTs to `update-task-status` with **Title Case** statuses and `result_data` as a structured dict (not JSON-stringified into `output_location`); `complete_task` is **never** invoked from anywhere in AA; `task-status` is **never** called by AA (it's a reader for reigh-app's poller).
3. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake claim response. Assert (a) JWKS verification gates the write — invalid jwt → `Failed`, (b) intent dispatch, (c) snapshot fast-path with and without `current_timeline`, (d) on success: `update_task_status(status='Complete', result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id})`, (e) on failure: `status='Failed'` with `error` and `result_data={correlation_id}`, (f) carry-fields on emitted clips, (g) `metadata.baseline_snapshot` location.
4. **Add** `tests/test_sequence_support.py`: a `SequenceDraft` with a known `clipType` materialises through the helper; an unrecognised `clipType` surfaces `UnsupportedSequenceError` and the worker reports `status='Failed'`.

### Step 15: Renderer parity smoke (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs` — Node ESM that imports fixtures from whichever subpath the local `@banodoco/timeline-schema` package actually exports (Phase 0 step 1 records this). If fixtures live in `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts` only and not in the local schema package, the helper instead reads them from a JSON file checked into `tests/fixtures/sprint08/` that was exported once via `tsx` against the reigh-app source. The plan does **not** depend on a specific package-subpath name; it depends on whatever the local packages export.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells to `node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, compares hash to `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/`. Skip with a documented reason if the helper or goldens are unavailable.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** — contract resolution + (optional) `config_version` PR + root `package.json` with `file:` deps.
2. **Phase 1** — renderer swap.
3. **Phase 2** — DataProvider with the canonical method surface, mutator-based retry against `update_timeline_config_versioned`, asset upload.
4. **Phase 3** — task-protocol client + JWKS verifier + worker (mirrors `banodoco-worker` reference image).
5. **Phase 4** — CLI collapse + node ops helper using direct timeline-ops primitives.
6. **Phase 5** — sequence support, with a documented fallback when public sequence APIs aren't exported by the local schema package.
7. **Phase 6** — parity + new unit tests.

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_task_client.py tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end `claim → JWKS verify → update_timeline_config_versioned → update-task-status(Complete, result_data) → reigh-app pollBanodocoTaskStatus` flow against staging.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py`.
5. After Phase 5: `pytest tests/test_sequence_support.py`.
6. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

Major mechanical shifts: (1) Worker no longer extends claim-next-task \u2014 the existing reigh-worker-orchestrator banodoco handler already implements the pool, AA mirrors banodoco-worker reference image. (2) Timeline write path switched from timeline-import edge function to update_timeline_config_versioned Postgres RPC (the versioned write the orchestrator handler uses). (3) JWKS verification of user_jwt added before terminal writes (new artagents/core/reigh/jwks.py). (4) Status reporting uses update-task-status's existing result_data: Record<string, unknown> field (NOT a JSON envelope inside output_location). result_data carries {config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} which the local task-status GET edge function (commit ee2e6f10c, sprint-8 branch) surfaces to pollBanodocoTaskStatus. AA never calls task-status. (5) Status enum corrected to Title Case (Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled), never lowercase. (6) Root package.json uses file: paths to /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript (the local packages are private and unpublished). (7) ops_helper.mjs uses direct timeline-ops primitives (addClip/moveClip/setTimelineTheme) instead of createTimelineCommandRunner (which lives in reigh-app source, not in the local timeline-ops package). (8) Sequence support documents a fallback path when validateSequenceDraft/applySequenceDraftToTimeline aren't exported by the local schema package. (9) Phase 0 cross-repo PR scope reduced to optional config_version extension only. DataProvider surface (resolution #6) was already correct in v3 and is preserved.",

        ### Task Summary
        Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform across six phases: (1) swap AA's hand-rolled Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, (2) build a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that POSTs to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read) with expected_version retry, (3) build a banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes and writes back via timeline-import, (4) collapse AA's parallel placement schema/CLI so verbs operate on reigh-app project IDs, (5) support SequenceDraft-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema, (6) add renderer parity tests using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve five open contracts (worker reception model, JWT/service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor 12 settled decisions from .megaplan/epic_bidirectional_editing.md.

Test must be rewritten to assert the new reigh-app-backed CLI verbs (list/show/edit via mocked reigh-data-fetch + timeline-import) with expected_version retry coverage.
