        ### Constraints
        - Do not introduce a new source: SourceRef|RunRef field on TimelineClip — SD-003 explicitly rejects this. Use the existing source_uuid + generation carry-fields instead.
- Do not reintroduce or extend the parallel placement schema (artagents/core/project/schema.py:build_placement and friends). SD-006 collapses it; sources/ and runs/ directories survive only as local provenance cache.
- Do not import from reigh-app internals. SD-010 mandates building against the public SDK at src/tools/video-editor/index.ts (sprint-08, @publicContract). DataProvider, TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError, fixture constructors come from there.
- Do not put the service-role key on the user-JWT path. SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the AA worker's final timeline-import write. Mirrors reigh-app's SD-022.
- Do not break tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py — byte-equivalent round-trip and allowlist parity with @banodoco/timeline-schema are gating invariants.
- Do not author one-way (AA composes only). SD-012 requires two-way authoring: both reigh-app and AA must be able to write.
- Do not skip the @banodoco/timeline-composition version reconcile. AA's v0.0.5 vs reigh-app sprint-08's local-path v0.0.2 must converge on a shared tag before parity tests are meaningful — flagged as a Phase 1 blocker by the doc.
- Do not silently bypass expected_version. SD-007: timeline-import push retries on conflict with cap N=3, surfaces failure to caller on exhaustion.
- Do not delete sources/ and runs/ directories during the placement-schema collapse. Per SD-006 they remain as local provenance cache; only the placement schema and local timeline.json-as-source-of-truth go.
- Do not lose AA-generated provenance on round-trip. New clips emitted via the worker must populate source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order so reigh-app's TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS allowlist preserves them. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 to AA's local run record for SD-008 drift detection.
- Do not invoke megaplan, the megaplan skill, or nested planning loops — explicit per harness directive.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Honour the SD-011 phase order, but front-load two discovery spikes before code so the worker design isn't rewritten twice. PHASE 0 (~half day, before Phase 1): freeze the @banodoco/timeline-composition version (cut a v0.0.6 tag both AA and reigh-app sprint-08 consume, OR temporarily switch AA to a workspace file:.. path matching reigh-app); resolve open contracts #1 (worker reception model — HTTP push vs poll-queue and ack envelope) and #4 (render lifecycle / realtime channel) by reading delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300 and pollBanodocoTaskStatus, document the answers in a top-of-module docstring on the new worker file. PHASE 1 — Renderer swap: rewrite remotion/src/Root.tsx to a ~30-line file: <Composition id=\"TimelineComposition\" component={TimelineComposition} defaultProps={...} calculateMetadata={({props}) => ({width, height, fps, durationInFrames: getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)})} />, importing from @banodoco/timeline-composition. Delete the AdosCard/AdosLowerThird/CornerLogo helpers — those become theme-package effects. Run remotion smoke; render a hype fixture; compare hash to a reigh-app golden if available, otherwise visual diff. PHASE 2 — Supabase client: add artagents/core/reigh/{env.py,supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py}. env.py extracts _candidate_env_files/_env_first from packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py into a shared helper. supabase_client.py wraps urllib.request POSTs with auth header selection (user JWT vs service-role per call site). data_provider.py implements a Python mirror of the sprint-08 DataProvider interface (fetch, persist, uploadAsset) calling reigh-data-fetch and timeline-import; raise TimelineVersionConflictError analogue on 409 and retry up to N=3 with re-fetch + re-apply (SD-007). Resolve open contract #3 (asset upload contract) by reading the render-outputs RLS migration and DataProvider.uploadAsset signature; pick a single bucket + signed-URL pattern. PHASE 3 — Worker: artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py exposes a polling loop or HTTP endpoint matching the answer from Phase 0 spike. It accepts the BanodocoTaskPayload envelope, dispatches by intent to existing executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc. via the orchestrator registry), writes back via the SupabaseDataProvider with the supplied correlation_id and expected_version, and emits status transitions queued -> running -> complete/failed via the resolved render-lifecycle channel. Add a `worker` subcommand to artagents/pipeline.py. Service-role key gates only the timeline-import write (SD-009). PHASE 4 — CLI collapse: rewrite artagents/core/project/cli.py to expose `list / show <uuid> / edit <uuid>` plus thin wrappers for add-clip / move-clip / set-theme that compose @banodoco/timeline-ops semantics in Python (port the small ops or call out to a node helper). Delete artagents/core/project/{timeline.py,materialize.py} and the placement symbols in schema.py; keep project.py, source.py, run.py for the local provenance cache (sources/, runs/). Update artagents/core/executor/cli.py so --project accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via SupabaseDataProvider. Rewrite tests/test_project_cli.py + delete or rewrite tests/test_project_materialize.py and test_project_schema.py. PHASE 5 — Sequence support: extend the worker to recognize SequenceDraft-shaped tasks; import the schema from banodoco_timeline_schema (or codegen Python types from the Zod source if missing); validate, then materialize via a small Python applySequenceDraftToTimeline equivalent or an out-of-process node call to the sprint-08 sequences module; fall back to an explicit \"unsupported sequence\" failure when the clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY (open contract #5 resolution). PHASE 6 — Parity tests: add tests/test_renderer_parity.py that exports the sprint-08 fixtures (createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture, createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture) to JSON via a small node helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and asserts hash match against a reigh-app golden. Add tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py, tests/test_banodoco_worker.py, tests/test_sequence_support.py for unit coverage. Update tests/test_open_in_reigh.py and tests/test_project_runs.py for SD-008 baseline_snapshot. Throughout, preserve source_uuid/generation/pool_id/clip_order on every clip the worker writes (SD-003) and never reintroduce a structured ref field. Document each open-contract resolution in the module docstring of the file that owns it so future agents see the why.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform: swap AA's Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, implement a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that talks to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read), build the banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes (correlation_id, expected_version, user_jwt) and writes back via timeline-import, collapse AA's CLI to operate on reigh-app project IDs (no more parallel placement schema), support sequence-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema's SequenceDraft, and verify renderer parity using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve the 5 open contracts (worker reception model, JWT+service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor the 12 settled decisions imported from the doc.

User notes and answers:
- CONTRACT RESOLUTION (post-cycle-3 escalation): The 4 cross-repo blockers from gate v3 have been resolved as follows. Plan revision should incorporate these as settled facts. (1) WORKER CLAIM PROTOCOL: Do NOT extend supabase/functions/claim-next-task. The orchestrator at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py ALREADY implements the banodoco worker-pool (BANODOCO_POOL constant, banodoco_timeline_generate task type, SD-034 envelope validation, JWKS-verified user_jwt, update_timeline_config_versioned write path, is_worker_pool_available check with BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC default 30s). AA implements the WORKER side of this pool — Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims banodoco_timeline_generate tasks, verifies user_jwt against Reigh's Supabase JWKS, executes, then writes via update_timeline_config_versioned (the versioned RPC). The reference Node+Python worker image is at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/ — AA can use that as the integration target. (2) TASK-STATUS ENDPOINT: The reigh-app sprint-8 branch (sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator) has been patched locally (commit ee2e6f10c, not yet pushed) to add a NEW supabase/functions/task-status/ edge function: GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>, JWT-authed via _shared/auth.ts, returns {status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}} sourced from tasks.result_data JSON column. This is exactly what pollBanodocoTaskStatus expects. AA does NOT need to call this endpoint itself — reigh-app's poller calls it. AA just writes result_data via update-task-status. (3) UPDATE-TASK-STATUS RESULT FIELD: update-task-status now accepts an optional result_data: Record<string, unknown> in its request body, persisted to tasks.result_data. AA's worker, on completion, calls update-task-status with status='Complete' and result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} so the new task-status reader can surface those fields. result_data column already existed in tasks (migration 20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql, jsonb default '{}'). (4) STATUS ENUM CASING: AA adapts to Title Case. Use Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled — these are the only values update-task-status accepts. Do NOT use lowercase. For AA's helper Node toolchain (Phase 1 fixture export, Phase 4 ops helpers), use file: dependencies in a root package.json pointing at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript. This works for sibling-checkout dev environments (which is the only deployment target right now). NPM publish is a separate deployment decision — out of scope for this megaplan. Helpers that need APIs not exported by the local packages (e.g., createTimelineCommandRunner from reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/commands/index.ts) should be replaced by direct timeline-ops calls (addClip/moveClip/setTimelineTheme/etc) since those primitives ARE exported. PHASE 0 of execute should add this root package.json and verify the file: imports work. (6) DATAPROVIDER METHOD LIST: Tighten Step 5's DataProvider Python interface to mirror the actual TypeScript contract: load_timeline, save_timeline, load_asset_registry, resolve_asset_url, optional register_asset, optional upload_asset, optional load_checkpoints/save_checkpoint, optional load_waveform (no save_waveform), optional load_asset_profile (no load_profile/save_profile). Remove fictional save_waveform/save_profile/load_profile from success criteria.
- CORRECTED FACTS (replan basis): (1) Package file: deps point at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema} — the package.json sits at the package ROOT, not /typescript subdir. (2) Worker write path uses SERVICE-ROLE Supabase client to call the versioned RPC; reference at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py. user_jwt is verified via JWKS for AUTHORIZATION (in worker_jwt.py) but the actual DB write is service-role. RPC signature: update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id uuid, p_expected_version int, p_config jsonb) — 3 params, no project_id. (3) Worker claim path: POST /functions/v1/claim-next-task with body {worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}.         Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform (v4)

## Overview

Goal unchanged: make AA a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform — renderer delegation to `<TimelineComposition>`, a Python `SupabaseDataProvider`, a `banodoco`-pool worker, CLI collapse, sequence-draft support, renderer parity tests.

v4 incorporates the post-cycle-3 contract resolutions provided in user notes (treated as settled facts):

- **Worker claim (resolution #1, CORRECTED)**: As of reigh-app commit `f8599a887` on `sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator` (local, not yet pushed), `supabase/functions/claim-next-task` now accepts `worker_pool: 'banodoco'`, `run_type: 'banodoco-worker'`, and `task_types: string[]`. The underlying RPC was extended via migration `20260504120000_extend_claim_next_task_for_pools.sql` (added 7-arg overload with `p_worker_pool TEXT DEFAULT NULL` and `p_task_types TEXT[] DEFAULT NULL`). AA's worker POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with `{worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}` — same shape as the reference `banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130`. The orchestrator at `reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` provides the `BANODOCO_POOL` taxonomy and `is_worker_pool_available` check. Reference Node+Python worker image at `banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/` is the integration target — AA can subclass or reimplement against the same contract.
- **task-status endpoint (resolution #2)**: A new `supabase/functions/task-status/` edge function already exists on the reigh-app sprint-8 branch (commit `ee2e6f10c`, not yet pushed). It is a `GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>`, JWT-authed, returning `{status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}}` sourced from `tasks.result_data`. AA does **not** call it — reigh-app's poller does. AA only needs to write `result_data` so the poller sees what it expects.
- **update-task-status result_data (resolution #3)**: `update-task-status` accepts optional `result_data: Record<string, unknown>` (persisted to `tasks.result_data`; column existed since migration `20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql`, jsonb default `'{}'`). On completion AA calls `update-task-status` with `status='Complete'` and `result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id}`. The new task-status reader surfaces those fields. The v3 "JSON envelope inside `output_location`" shim is replaced by this first-class field.
- **Status enum casing (resolution #4)**: Use **Title Case** — `Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled`. Lowercase is rejected.
- **Package deps (resolution #5, CORRECTED)**: `@banodoco/timeline-ops` and `@banodoco/timeline-schema` are private and unpublished. AA's root `package.json` uses `file:` dependencies pointing at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops` and `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema` — the package.json sits at the package ROOT, not in a `/typescript` subdirectory. `file:.../timeline-ops/typescript` would fail because no manifest exists at that path. This is the only deployment target for now (sibling-checkout dev environment); npm publish is out of scope. APIs not exported by the local packages (`createTimelineCommandRunner` lives in `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/commands/index.ts`, not in `timeline-ops`) are replaced by **direct timeline-ops primitives** that ARE exported (`addClip`, `moveClip`, `setTimelineTheme`, etc.).
- **DataProvider surface (resolution #6)**: Required `load_timeline`, `save_timeline`, `load_asset_registry`, `resolve_asset_url`. Optional `register_asset`, `upload_asset`, `load_checkpoints`/`save_checkpoint`, `load_waveform` (no `save_waveform`), `load_asset_profile` (no `load_profile`/`save_profile`). Carried over from v3.

All v3 settled decisions still hold (mutator-based retry, expected_version required, asset upload to `timeline-assets`, baseline_snapshot at `runs/<run_id>.json#metadata.baseline_snapshot`, two-way authoring). Two of those are now mechanically different and CORRECTED from v3:

- **Timeline write path**: AA writes via the `update_timeline_config_versioned` Postgres RPC. Reference: `banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py`. The RPC signature is `update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id uuid, p_expected_version int, p_config jsonb)` — three params, no project_id. The mutator-based retry (`save_timeline(mutator, retries=3)`) wraps the RPC; on version mismatch retry up to N=3, mapping to `TimelineVersionConflictError`.
- **Auth (CORRECTED)**: The DB write uses a **service-role Supabase client** to call `update_timeline_config_versioned` (per `worker_writes.py`). The user_jwt from the claim envelope is verified against Reigh's Supabase JWKS (per `worker_jwt.py`) for **authorization** (confirming the user owns the timeline) but is NOT used for the database call itself. This matches the reference banodoco-worker pattern. AA stores the service-role key in `REIGH_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` env var, mirroring banodoco-worker.
- **Sequence support DROPPED from this sprint**: `timeline-schema` does NOT export `SequenceDraft`, `validateSequenceDraft`, or `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` — those live in `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/sequences/` which is not a published package. Phase 5 (sequence support) is removed from this sprint and deferred until those modules are published or otherwise consumable by AA. AA's worker only handles `banodoco_timeline_generate` tasks (clip-level), not sequence-shaped tasks, in this sprint.

Repository shape that matters (additions in v4 marked):
- `package.json` (**new at repo root**, `file:` deps), `scripts/node/{ops_helper.mjs,sequence_helper.mjs,export_fixtures.mjs}` (**new**).
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx`, `remotion/package.json`, `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/{timeline-composition,timeline-schema}/**`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}`.
- `artagents/core/reigh/{__init__.py,env.py,errors.py,supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,data_provider.py,task_client.py,sequences.py,jwks.py}` (**new** — `jwks.py` is added for SD-009 user_jwt verification).
- `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py` (**new**).
- `artagents/timeline.py`, `artagents/packs/builtin/{reigh_data,open_in_reigh}/run.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`.
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py`, plus new test files.

## Phase 0: Discovery, Tooling, No Cross-Repo PRs Required

### Step 1: Capture cross-repo contracts and write the integration brief (`docs/integration_contracts.md`)
**Scope:** Small (research + doc)
1. **Read** and quote line ranges into `docs/integration_contracts.md` (new):
   - `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` — `BANODOCO_POOL`, `banodoco_timeline_generate`, SD-034 envelope, `update_timeline_config_versioned`, `is_worker_pool_available`, `BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC`.
   - `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/task_utils.py` — claim/update primitives AA mirrors.
   - `../banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/` — reference Node+Python worker image (the integration target shape).
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/task-status/index.ts` (commit `ee2e6f10c`, sprint-8 branch) — GET response shape: `{status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}}`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/update-task-status/{index.ts,types.ts,request.ts}` — confirms `result_data: Record<string, unknown>` field, status enum `Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled`.
   - `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` — `TIMELINES_SELECT` columns; if `config_version` is missing, that single small change is the only cross-repo PR scope (see Step 2).
   - `../reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/data/{DataProvider.ts,SupabaseDataProvider.ts,AssetResolver.ts}` — canonical method surface, `timeline-assets` bucket layout.
   - `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops/typescript/src/index.ts` — exports `addClip`, `moveClip`, `setTimelineTheme`, etc. (no `createTimelineCommandRunner`).
   - `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema/typescript/src/index.ts` — exports `schemas`, `resolveTheme`, etc. (Phase 5 records the actual sequence/testing exports).
2. **Document** in `docs/integration_contracts.md` the resolutions for the five open contracts (now grounded in the resolution notes):
   - **#1 Worker reception** — AA mirrors the existing banodoco-worker pattern (poll the orchestrator's claim endpoint with the same shape `task_utils` uses; the orchestrator already filters for `BANODOCO_POOL`).
   - **#2 JWT/service-role** — claim envelope carries a JWKS-verifiable user_jwt; AA verifies via Reigh's Supabase JWKS, uses that JWT against `update_timeline_config_versioned`. Service-role only on the orchestrator-internal task endpoints.
   - **#3 Asset upload** — `timeline-assets` bucket, `${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}`, then `register_asset`.
   - **#4 Render lifecycle** — AA writes `result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id}` via `update-task-status` with status `Complete`. The pre-existing `task-status` GET edge function (commit `ee2e6f10c`) surfaces `result_data` fields to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
   - **#5 Sequence schema** — record the exact symbols available on the local `@banodoco/timeline-schema` package; if the trusted-sequence/applySequenceDraft APIs are not yet exported, Phase 5 falls back to validating against `banodoco_timeline_schema`'s JSON Schema and emits clips through ordinary `timeline-ops` primitives. No invented imports.
3. **Reconcile** the three shared packages. Pin matching versions in `remotion/package.json` and the new root `package.json`. Strategy is recorded in the doc.

### Step 2: Single small reigh-app PR if `config_version` is missing from `reigh-data-fetch` (optional)
**Scope:** Small
1. **If** `TIMELINES_SELECT` in `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` does not already include `config_version`, open a small PR adding it. Otherwise skip — Phase 0 step 1 confirms whether this is needed. No claim-next-task or update-task-status changes are required (resolutions #1–#3 confirm the surfaces already exist).

### Step 3: Add a root Node toolchain for helpers (`package.json`, `scripts/node/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `package.json` at the AA repo root:
   - `"private": true`, `"type": "module"`.
   - `dependencies`:
     - `"@banodoco/timeline-ops": "file:/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops/typescript"`
     - `"@banodoco/timeline-schema": "file:/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema/typescript"`
   - `scripts`: `"ops": "node scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs"`, `"sequence": "node scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs"`, `"fixtures": "node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs"`.
2. **Run** `npm install` at the repo root; verify the `file:` paths resolve and the helpers can `import { addClip, moveClip, setTimelineTheme } from '@banodoco/timeline-ops'`. Commit `package-lock.json`.
3. **Document** in `docs/integration_contracts.md` that this is sibling-checkout-only for now; npm publish is out of scope.

## Phase 1: Renderer Swap

### Step 4: Replace hand-rolled Remotion comp with `<TimelineComposition>` (`remotion/src/Root.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `remotion/src/Root.tsx` to a thin `<Composition>` wrapper that imports `TimelineComposition` and `getTimelineDurationInFrames` from `@banodoco/timeline-composition`. Forward `defaultProps`/`calculateMetadata`. Delete local `RenderClip`, `AdosCard`, `AdosLowerThird`, `CornerLogo`, and local `TimelineClip`/`TimelineConfig`/`AssetRegistry` types.
2. **Pull** the existing `defaultProps` JSON shape forward unchanged. `calculateMetadata` derives `width`/`height`/`fps`/`durationInFrames` via `getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)`.
3. **Verify** with `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` (or `node remotion/__smoke__/bundle.mjs`); render `examples/hype.timeline.full.json` headless.

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + DataProvider Surface (Tightened)

### Step 5: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move env helpers (`_read_env_value`, `_candidate_env_files`, `_env_first`, `resolve_api_url`, `resolve_pat`) from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105`.
2. **Add** `resolve_orchestrator_url(...)`, `resolve_supabase_url(...)`, `resolve_service_role_key(...)`, `resolve_jwks_url(...)`. The `update-task-status` URL derives from the orchestrator/Supabase URL.
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export from `artagents.core.reigh.env` so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing.

### Step 6: Implement `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the canonical TS surface exactly (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,errors.py,data_provider.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError`, `TimelineNotFoundError`, `UnsupportedSequenceError`.
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin POST/RPC wrapper. Auth selector parameterised: `auth=("user_jwt", token)` (default) or `auth=("service_role", key)`. `update_timeline_config_versioned` is called as a Supabase RPC; a version-mismatch result maps to `TimelineVersionConflictError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_io.py` with the mutator-based save:

   ```python
   def save_timeline(
       *,
       project_id: str,
       timeline_id: str,
       mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig],
       auth: Auth,
       retries: int = 3,
       force: bool = False,
   ) -> SaveResult: ...
   ```

   Loop: load `(timeline, config_version)` via `reigh-data-fetch`, apply mutator, call `update_timeline_config_versioned(project_id, timeline_id, new_config, expected_version=config_version)`. On version-mismatch result, re-fetch + re-apply, up to `retries`. Reject `expected_version=None` on the normal path; only `force=True` bypasses, and it logs.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` whose method surface matches `data/DataProvider.ts` exactly:
   - **Required**: `load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> tuple[TimelineConfig, int]`, `save_timeline(...)` (wraps `timeline_io.save_timeline`), `load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id) -> AssetRegistry`, `resolve_asset_url(asset_entry) -> str`.
   - **Optional**: `register_asset(...)`, `upload_asset(local_path_or_bytes, *, content_type, user_id, timeline_id, filename) -> AssetEntry` (uploads to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}`, then `register_asset`), `load_checkpoints(...)`, `save_checkpoint(...)`, `load_waveform(asset_id)`, `load_asset_profile(asset_id)`.
   - **Explicitly NOT** present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile`.
5. **Round-trip** every fetched timeline through `artagents.timeline.validate_timeline` / `load_timeline` / `save_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.

### Step 7: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy path with `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., mutator=lambda t, v: merged_timeline)`. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` to mock the DataProvider on the default path.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker (Mirrors banodoco-worker reference image)

### Step 8: Implement task-protocol client and JWKS verifier (`artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/jwks.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py` wrapping the **actual** task endpoints used by the existing orchestrator handler:
   - `claim_next_task(auth=("service_role", key)) -> ClaimResult | None` — POSTs to the orchestrator's claim endpoint with the same shape `task_utils` uses, filtered to `task_type='banodoco_timeline_generate'`. Returns the SD-034 envelope (`{ task_id, run_id, project_id, params, user_jwt, ... }`) or `None` if empty.
   - `update_task_status(task_id, *, status, result_data=None, error=None, auth=("service_role", key))` — POSTs to `update-task-status` with `status ∈ {Queued, In Progress, Complete, Failed, Cancelled}` (Title Case) and optional `result_data: dict` (persisted into `tasks.result_data`).
2. **Document** in the module docstring that AA never calls `complete_task` (file/storage shape) and never calls `task-status` (that's reigh-app's `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reader).
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/jwks.py` — verifies a user_jwt against the JWKS published at `resolve_jwks_url()`. Used to gate the worker's terminal write.

### Step 9: Implement worker (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. Module docstring records the resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with file:line citations to `../reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py` and the local `task-status/index.ts` (commit `ee2e6f10c`).
2. **Reception**: long-running poll loop calling `task_client.claim_next_task(auth=service_role)`. The orchestrator already filters by `BANODOCO_POOL` / `task_type='banodoco_timeline_generate'`; AA defensively rejects any envelope whose `task_type` is not `banodoco_timeline_generate`.
3. **JWKS verification**: before any timeline write, verify `task.user_jwt` via `jwks.verify(task.user_jwt)`. Reject with `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt')` and skip on failure.
4. **Snapshot fast-path**: if `params.current_timeline` is present, dispatch using it directly (skip `load_timeline`). Otherwise call `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id)`.
5. **Dispatch by `intent`** to existing AA executors. Each executor returns a mutator callable.
6. **Carry-fields**: every emitted clip populates `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003).
7. **Local provenance**: write `runs/<run_id>.json` with `metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload))` (SD-008).
8. **Timeline write**: `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(project_id, timeline_id, mutator, auth=("user_jwt", task.user_jwt), retries=3)`. Internally this calls the `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC. `expected_version=params.expected_version` is required; `force=True` is forbidden.
9. **Status report on success**: `task_client.update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': timeline_id}, auth=service_role)`. The new `task-status` reader surfaces these fields to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
10. **Status report on failure**: `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id})`. Title Case, never lowercase.
11. **Wire** `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help`.

## Phase 4: CLI Collapse — Operate on reigh-app Project UUIDs

### Step 10: Delete the placement schema (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,project.py}`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Delete** `artagents/core/project/timeline.py` and `artagents/core/project/materialize.py`.
2. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/schema.py`: remove placement symbols. Keep project/source/run schema constants.
3. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/project.py`: stop writing `timeline.json` from `create_project`; delete `load_project_timeline`/`save_project_timeline`. `show_project` either fetches via `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline` (if `project.json#project_id` is set) or reports cache-only state.
4. **Keep** `source.py`, `run.py`, `paths.py`, `jsonio.py`. Add an opaque `project_id` field on `project.json`.

### Step 11: Rewrite the `projects` CLI surface using direct timeline-ops primitives (`artagents/core/project/cli.py`, `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `artagents/core/project/cli.py` so subcommands are: `create`, `show`, `source add`, `list <project_id>`, `edit <project_id>`. Drop placement verbs.
2. **Create** `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` (Node ESM) that imports **direct primitives** from `@banodoco/timeline-ops` (the local file: package): `import { addClip, moveClip, setTimelineTheme } from '@banodoco/timeline-ops';`. The helper reads `{timeline, version, op, args}` JSON from stdin, dispatches by `op` to the right primitive, applies it, and writes the resulting timeline to stdout. **No** `createTimelineCommandRunner` (it doesn't exist in the local package).
3. **Wire** edit verbs (`add-clip`, `move-clip`, `set-theme`) so they shell to `node scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` and use the resulting timeline as `mutator`'s return value inside `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(..., retries=3)`. `expected_version` is required.
4. **Update** `artagents/core/executor/cli.py:_cmd_run` so `--project` accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via `SupabaseDataProvider`. Keep the slug path as cache-only/offline mode.

### Step 12: Rewrite project-CLI tests (`tests/test_project_cli.py`, `tests/test_project_materialize.py`, `tests/test_project_schema.py`, `tests/test_project_runs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_*` against mocked `reigh-data-fetch` + `update_timeline_config_versioned` (force a version-mismatch and assert the mutator is re-applied; assert exhaustion at N=3; assert that omitting `expected_version` raises rather than force-saving).
2. **Delete** `tests/test_project_materialize.py` (placement-only).
3. **Trim** `tests/test_project_schema.py` to project/source/run schema only.
4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to assert `metadata.baseline_snapshot` is a sha256 hex string at exactly that path.

## Phase 5: Sequence Support (against actual local-package exports)

### Step 13: Sequence helper + worker wiring (`scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`, `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Inspect** `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema/typescript/src/` (Phase 0 step 1 records what's actually there). Two cases:
   - **If** `validateSequenceDraft` / `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` / `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` are exported, `scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs` imports and uses them.
   - **If not**, the helper validates the SequenceDraft against the local JSON Schema (`schemas` export) and emits the resulting clips through ordinary `timeline-ops` primitives (`addClip` per clip in the draft). Phase 0 records this fallback decision.
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py` that shells to the helper and returns a mutator usable by `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline`. If the helper reports an unsupported `clipType`, raise `UnsupportedSequenceError`.
3. **Detect** sequence-shaped intents in the worker: when `params.intent` carries a `SequenceDraft`, run `sequences.validate(...)`. On `UnsupportedSequenceError`, complete the task with `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error=f'unsupported sequence: {clipType}')`.

## Phase 6: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 14: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock the Supabase client. Assert (a) `load_timeline` returns `(config, config_version)` and round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `save_timeline` re-applies the mutator on RPC version-mismatch and exhausts at N=3, (c) `save_timeline` raises when `expected_version` is omitted unless `force=True`, (d) `update_timeline_config_versioned` only ever sees `auth=("user_jwt", ...)` (never service-role), (e) `upload_asset` writes to `timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then calls `register_asset`, (f) the DataProvider surface is exactly the canonical set (presence + absence tests).
2. **Add** `tests/test_task_client.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert `claim_next_task` POSTs the orchestrator claim envelope; `update_task_status` POSTs to `update-task-status` with **Title Case** statuses and `result_data` as a structured dict (not JSON-stringified into `output_location`); `complete_task` is **never** invoked from anywhere in AA; `task-status` is **never** called by AA (it's a reader for reigh-app's poller).
3. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake claim response. Assert (a) JWKS verification gates the write — invalid jwt → `Failed`, (b) intent dispatch, (c) snapshot fast-path with and without `current_timeline`, (d) on success: `update_task_status(status='Complete', result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id})`, (e) on failure: `status='Failed'` with `error` and `result_data={correlation_id}`, (f) carry-fields on emitted clips, (g) `metadata.baseline_snapshot` location.
4. **Add** `tests/test_sequence_support.py`: a `SequenceDraft` with a known `clipType` materialises through the helper; an unrecognised `clipType` surfaces `UnsupportedSequenceError` and the worker reports `status='Failed'`.

### Step 15: Renderer parity smoke (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs` — Node ESM that imports fixtures from whichever subpath the local `@banodoco/timeline-schema` package actually exports (Phase 0 step 1 records this). If fixtures live in `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts` only and not in the local schema package, the helper instead reads them from a JSON file checked into `tests/fixtures/sprint08/` that was exported once via `tsx` against the reigh-app source. The plan does **not** depend on a specific package-subpath name; it depends on whatever the local packages export.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells to `node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, compares hash to `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/`. Skip with a documented reason if the helper or goldens are unavailable.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** — contract resolution + (optional) `config_version` PR + root `package.json` with `file:` deps.
2. **Phase 1** — renderer swap.
3. **Phase 2** — DataProvider with the canonical method surface, mutator-based retry against `update_timeline_config_versioned`, asset upload.
4. **Phase 3** — task-protocol client + JWKS verifier + worker (mirrors `banodoco-worker` reference image).
5. **Phase 4** — CLI collapse + node ops helper using direct timeline-ops primitives.
6. **Phase 5** — sequence support, with a documented fallback when public sequence APIs aren't exported by the local schema package.
7. **Phase 6** — parity + new unit tests.

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_task_client.py tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end `claim → JWKS verify → update_timeline_config_versioned → update-task-status(Complete, result_data) → reigh-app pollBanodocoTaskStatus` flow against staging.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py`.
5. After Phase 5: `pytest tests/test_sequence_support.py`.
6. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

        Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform across six phases: (1) swap AA's hand-rolled Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, (2) build a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that POSTs to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read) with expected_version retry, (3) build a banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes and writes back via timeline-import, (4) collapse AA's parallel placement schema/CLI so verbs operate on reigh-app project IDs, (5) support SequenceDraft-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema, (6) add renderer parity tests using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve five open contracts (worker reception model, JWT/service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor 12 settled decisions from .megaplan/epic_bidirectional_editing.md.

        ### Key Evidence
        - AA's renderer is a 496-line hand-rolled <TimelineComposition> in remotion/src/Root.tsx that ignores the already-installed @banodoco/timeline-composition v0.0.5 package. The package exports a real <TimelineComposition> from typescript/src/TimelineComposition.tsx with EFFECT_REGISTRY/CLIP_TYPE_ALIASES dispatch, ThemeProvider, AudioTrack/VisualClip, and getTimelineDurationInFrames — the swap drops the hand-rolled clip rendering and delegates entirely. - Version reconcile is required before parity tests are meaningful: AA pins @banodoco/timeline-composition v0.0.5 (GitHub archive tag); reigh-app sprint-08 uses a local file path at v0.0.2. Brief explicitly flags this — must ship a fresh tag both sides consume or AA reverts to local path during dev. (source: .megaplan/epic_bidirectional_editing.md (Phase 1 'Reconcile @banodoco/timeline-composition versioning'); relevance: high)
- AA's project subsystem is built around a parallel placement schema that SD-006 says to collapse. artagents/core/project/schema.py defines build_project_timeline/build_placement/validate_project_timeline keyed on a local timeline.json with a placements[] list and source/run REF_KINDS. artagents/core/project/materialize.py converts placements -> Banodoco TimelineConfig. artagents/core/project/cli.py exposes create/source add/timeline place-source/place-run/remove/materialize.         - [pass_to_pass] remotion/__smoke__/bundle.mjs (smoke): Remotion bundle compiles after Root.tsx is reduced to a <TimelineComposition> delegation. Run `npm -w remotion run smoke` after Phase 1.

        ### Constraints
        - Do not introduce a new source: SourceRef|RunRef field on TimelineClip — SD-003 explicitly rejects this. Use the existing source_uuid + generation carry-fields instead.
- Do not reintroduce or extend the parallel placement schema (artagents/core/project/schema.py:build_placement and friends). SD-006 collapses it; sources/ and runs/ directories survive only as local provenance cache.
- Do not import from reigh-app internals. SD-010 mandates building against the public SDK at src/tools/video-editor/index.ts (sprint-08, @publicContract). DataProvider, TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError, fixture constructors come from there.
- Do not put the service-role key on the user-JWT path. SD-009: user JWT for ownership-bound CLI calls; service-role only for the AA worker's final timeline-import write. Mirrors reigh-app's SD-022.
- Do not break tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py — byte-equivalent round-trip and allowlist parity with @banodoco/timeline-schema are gating invariants.
- Do not author one-way (AA composes only). SD-012 requires two-way authoring: both reigh-app and AA must be able to write.
- Do not skip the @banodoco/timeline-composition version reconcile. AA's v0.0.5 vs reigh-app sprint-08's local-path v0.0.2 must converge on a shared tag before parity tests are meaningful — flagged as a Phase 1 blocker by the doc.
- Do not silently bypass expected_version. SD-007: timeline-import push retries on conflict with cap N=3, surfaces failure to caller on exhaustion.
- Do not delete sources/ and runs/ directories during the placement-schema collapse. Per SD-006 they remain as local provenance cache; only the placement schema and local timeline.json-as-source-of-truth go.
- Do not lose AA-generated provenance on round-trip. New clips emitted via the worker must populate source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order so reigh-app's TIMELINE_CLIP_FIELDS allowlist preserves them. Add baseline_snapshot sha256 to AA's local run record for SD-008 drift detection.
- Do not invoke megaplan, the megaplan skill, or nested planning loops — explicit per harness directive.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Honour the SD-011 phase order, but front-load two discovery spikes before code so the worker design isn't rewritten twice. PHASE 0 (~half day, before Phase 1): freeze the @banodoco/timeline-composition version (cut a v0.0.6 tag both AA and reigh-app sprint-08 consume, OR temporarily switch AA to a workspace file:.. path matching reigh-app); resolve open contracts #1 (worker reception model — HTTP push vs poll-queue and ack envelope) and #4 (render lifecycle / realtime channel) by reading delegateToBanodocoAgent.ts:160-300 and pollBanodocoTaskStatus, document the answers in a top-of-module docstring on the new worker file. PHASE 1 — Renderer swap: rewrite remotion/src/Root.tsx to a ~30-line file: <Composition id=\"TimelineComposition\" component={TimelineComposition} defaultProps={...} calculateMetadata={({props}) => ({width, height, fps, durationInFrames: getTimelineDurationInFrames(props.timeline, fps)})} />, importing from @banodoco/timeline-composition. Delete the AdosCard/AdosLowerThird/CornerLogo helpers — those become theme-package effects. Run remotion smoke; render a hype fixture; compare hash to a reigh-app golden if available, otherwise visual diff. PHASE 2 — Supabase client: add artagents/core/reigh/{env.py,supabase_client.py,data_provider.py,timeline_import.py}. env.py extracts _candidate_env_files/_env_first from packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py into a shared helper. supabase_client.py wraps urllib.request POSTs with auth header selection (user JWT vs service-role per call site). data_provider.py implements a Python mirror of the sprint-08 DataProvider interface (fetch, persist, uploadAsset) calling reigh-data-fetch and timeline-import; raise TimelineVersionConflictError analogue on 409 and retry up to N=3 with re-fetch + re-apply (SD-007). Resolve open contract #3 (asset upload contract) by reading the render-outputs RLS migration and DataProvider.uploadAsset signature; pick a single bucket + signed-URL pattern. PHASE 3 — Worker: artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py exposes a polling loop or HTTP endpoint matching the answer from Phase 0 spike. It accepts the BanodocoTaskPayload envelope, dispatches by intent to existing executors (hype, logo_ideas, etc. via the orchestrator registry), writes back via the SupabaseDataProvider with the supplied correlation_id and expected_version, and emits status transitions queued -> running -> complete/failed via the resolved render-lifecycle channel. Add a `worker` subcommand to artagents/pipeline.py. Service-role key gates only the timeline-import write (SD-009). PHASE 4 — CLI collapse: rewrite artagents/core/project/cli.py to expose `list / show <uuid> / edit <uuid>` plus thin wrappers for add-clip / move-clip / set-theme that compose @banodoco/timeline-ops semantics in Python (port the small ops or call out to a node helper). Delete artagents/core/project/{timeline.py,materialize.py} and the placement symbols in schema.py; keep project.py, source.py, run.py for the local provenance cache (sources/, runs/). Update artagents/core/executor/cli.py so --project accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via SupabaseDataProvider. Rewrite tests/test_project_cli.py + delete or rewrite tests/test_project_materialize.py and test_project_schema.py. PHASE 5 — Sequence support: extend the worker to recognize SequenceDraft-shaped tasks; import the schema from banodoco_timeline_schema (or codegen Python types from the Zod source if missing); validate, then materialize via a small Python applySequenceDraftToTimeline equivalent or an out-of-process node call to the sprint-08 sequences module; fall back to an explicit \"unsupported sequence\" failure when the clipType is not in LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY / THEME_PACKAGE_REGISTRY (open contract #5 resolution). PHASE 6 — Parity tests: add tests/test_renderer_parity.py that exports the sprint-08 fixtures (createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture, createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture) to JSON via a small node helper, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, and asserts hash match against a reigh-app golden. Add tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py, tests/test_banodoco_worker.py, tests/test_sequence_support.py for unit coverage. Update tests/test_open_in_reigh.py and tests/test_project_runs.py for SD-008 baseline_snapshot. Throughout, preserve source_uuid/generation/pool_id/clip_order on every clip the worker writes (SD-003) and never reintroduce a structured ref field. Document each open-contract resolution in the module docstring of the file that owns it so future agents see the why.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform: swap AA's Remotion comp to <TimelineComposition> from @banodoco/timeline-composition, implement a Python DataProvider + Supabase client that talks to timeline-import (write) and reigh-data-fetch (read), build the banodoco worker that consumes delegateToBanodocoAgent task envelopes (correlation_id, expected_version, user_jwt) and writes back via timeline-import, collapse AA's CLI to operate on reigh-app project IDs (no more parallel placement schema), support sequence-shaped tasks via @banodoco/timeline-schema's SequenceDraft, and verify renderer parity using sprint-08's createAgentWorkflowTimelineFixture/createEmbedDemoTimelineFixture. Resolve the 5 open contracts (worker reception model, JWT+service-role lifecycle, asset upload contract, render lifecycle, sequence schema) during execution. Honor the 12 settled decisions imported from the doc.

User notes and answers:
- CONTRACT RESOLUTION (post-cycle-3 escalation): The 4 cross-repo blockers from gate v3 have been resolved as follows. Plan revision should incorporate these as settled facts. (1) WORKER CLAIM PROTOCOL: Do NOT extend supabase/functions/claim-next-task. The orchestrator at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py ALREADY implements the banodoco worker-pool (BANODOCO_POOL constant, banodoco_timeline_generate task type, SD-034 envelope validation, JWKS-verified user_jwt, update_timeline_config_versioned write path, is_worker_pool_available check with BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC default 30s). AA implements the WORKER side of this pool — Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims banodoco_timeline_generate tasks, verifies user_jwt against Reigh's Supabase JWKS, executes, then writes via update_timeline_config_versioned (the versioned RPC). The reference Node+Python worker image is at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/ — AA can use that as the integration target. (2) TASK-STATUS ENDPOINT: The reigh-app sprint-8 branch (sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator) has been patched locally (commit ee2e6f10c, not yet pushed) to add a NEW supabase/functions/task-status/ edge function: GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>, JWT-authed via _shared/auth.ts, returns {status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}} sourced from tasks.result_data JSON column. This is exactly what pollBanodocoTaskStatus expects. AA does NOT need to call this endpoint itself — reigh-app's poller calls it. AA just writes result_data via update-task-status. (3) UPDATE-TASK-STATUS RESULT FIELD: update-task-status now accepts an optional result_data: Record<string, unknown> in its request body, persisted to tasks.result_data. AA's worker, on completion, calls update-task-status with status='Complete' and result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} so the new task-status reader can surface those fields. result_data column already existed in tasks (migration 20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql, jsonb default '{}'). (4) STATUS ENUM CASING: AA adapts to Title Case. Use Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled — these are the only values update-task-status accepts. Do NOT use lowercase. (5) PACKAGE DEPS: @banodoco/timeline-ops and @banodoco/timeline-schema are private and unpublished. For AA's helper Node toolchain (Phase 1 fixture export, Phase 4 ops helpers), use file: dependencies in a root package.json pointing at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript. This works for sibling-checkout dev environments (which is the only deployment target right now). NPM publish is a separate deployment decision — out of scope for this megaplan. Helpers that need APIs not exported by the local packages (e.g., createTimelineCommandRunner from reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/commands/index.ts) should be replaced by direct timeline-ops calls (addClip/moveClip/setTimelineTheme/etc) since those primitives ARE exported. PHASE 0 of execute should add this root package.json and verify the file: imports work. (6) DATAPROVIDER METHOD LIST: Tighten Step 5's DataProvider Python interface to mirror the actual TypeScript contract: load_timeline, save_timeline, load_asset_registry, resolve_asset_url, optional register_asset, optional upload_asset, optional load_checkpoints/save_checkpoint, optional load_waveform (no save_waveform), optional load_asset_profile (no load_profile/save_profile). Remove fictional save_waveform/save_profile/load_profile from success criteria.
- CORRECTED FACTS (replan basis): (1) Package file: deps point at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema} — the package.json sits at the package ROOT, not /typescript subdir. (2) Worker write path uses SERVICE-ROLE Supabase client to call the versioned RPC; reference at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py. user_jwt is verified via JWKS for AUTHORIZATION (in worker_jwt.py) but the actual DB write is service-role. RPC signature: update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id uuid, p_expected_version int, p_config jsonb) — 3 params, no project_id. (3) Worker claim path: POST /functions/v1/claim-next-task with body {worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}. As of commit f8599a887 on reigh-app sprint-8 branch, this is now supported. Reference at banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130. (4) Worker status report path: POST /functions/v1/update-task-status with {task_id, status:'Complete'|'Failed'|..., output_location?, error?, result_data?:{config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id, ...}}. Status values are Title Case. result_data is a new optional jsonb field as of commit ee2e6f10c. (5) DROP SEQUENCE SUPPORT FROM THIS SPRINT. timeline-schema does NOT export SequenceDraft/validateSequenceDraft/TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES — those live in reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/sequences/ which is not a published package. AA's Phase 5 should be removed. Sequence support comes in a later sprint after that module is published or otherwise made consumable by AA. (6) Reference banodoco-worker module structure at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/: worker.py (claim loop), worker_writes.py (RPC), worker_jwt.py (JWKS verify), worker_assets.py, worker_pipeline.py, worker_remotion.py, worker_health.py. AA's worker can either subclass/extend this reference or reimplement against the same contract. (7) DataProvider TS contract has only loadWaveform?(assetId) and loadAssetProfile?(assetId) — no save_waveform, no load_profile, no save_profile. Tighten the Python interface accordingly. (8) Phase plan: Phase 1: Renderer swap (AA's remotion/src/Root.tsx delegates to <TimelineComposition>); Phase 2: Python DataProvider client (timeline-import write, reigh-data-fetch read, optimistic versioning); Phase 3: AA banodoco worker (claim loop, JWKS verify, dispatch banodoco_timeline_generate to AA executor, write via service-role RPC, status via update-task-status); Phase 4: CLI collapse (artagents project verbs operate on reigh-app project IDs via DataProvider); Phase 5 (REMOVED). Phase 6: Renderer parity smoke tests using sprint-8 fixtures.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire ArtAgents into reigh-app's sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform (v5)

## Overview

Goal unchanged: make AA a peer of reigh-app on the sprint-08 bidirectional editing platform — renderer delegation to `<TimelineComposition>`, a Python `SupabaseDataProvider`, a `banodoco`-pool worker, CLI collapse, renderer parity tests. **Sequence support is deferred to a later sprint** (Phase 5 removed) — `timeline-schema` does not yet export `SequenceDraft` / `validateSequenceDraft` / `applySequenceDraftToTimeline` / `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES`, so the worker only handles `banodoco_timeline_generate` clip-level tasks in this sprint.

v5 is a mechanical cleanup of v4: the v4 overview captured the user-supplied corrected facts, but Step 3, Step 6, Step 9, Phase 5, and the success criteria still carried v3 wording. v5 propagates the corrections through the entire plan body so the executor can follow it without internal contradictions.

Key v5 corrections (all settled facts from user notes, verified against the filesystem):

- **Package `file:` paths**: point at the package ROOTS where `package.json` actually lives — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops` and `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema`. The `/typescript` subpaths have no `package.json` and would fail to install.
- **Versioned RPC signature**: `update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id uuid, p_expected_version integer, p_config jsonb)` — three params, no `project_id`. Verified against `../reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260326100000_add_timeline_config_version.sql`.
- **Worker write auth**: AA mirrors the reference `banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py` exactly. The DB call uses a **service-role Supabase client** (env: `REIGH_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`). The user_jwt from the claim envelope is JWKS-verified by `worker_jwt.py` for **authorization** (confirming the user owns the timeline), but the actual RPC invocation is service-role. SD-009's "service-role power gated to the worker" intent survives via this exact mechanism. AA never uses service-role against the user-facing edge functions (`timeline-import` is not used at all in this sprint; reads via `reigh-data-fetch` use a PAT or user JWT).
- **Claim protocol**: AA POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with body `{worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}`. Reference: `banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130`. As of reigh-app commit `f8599a887` (sprint-8 branch, local), the `claim-next-task` edge function accepts this shape (extended via migration `20260504120000_extend_claim_next_task_for_pools.sql`). The plan does **not** mirror generic `task_utils.py` (which historically only takes `worker_id`/`run_type`).
- **`config_version` from `reigh-data-fetch` is REQUIRED**: `TIMELINES_SELECT` in `../reigh-app/supabase/functions/reigh-data-fetch/index.ts` is verified missing `config_version`. Phase 0 Step 2 ships a small reigh-app PR adding it. This is a hard prerequisite for Phase 2; not optional.
- **Sequence support deferred**: Phase 5, `scripts/node/sequence_helper.mjs`, `artagents/core/reigh/sequences.py`, `UnsupportedSequenceError`, `tests/test_sequence_support.py`, and the sequence success criterion are all removed.
- **Status reporting**: `update-task-status` with Title Case statuses (`Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled`) and structured `result_data: dict` carrying `{config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id}`. The pre-existing local `task-status` GET edge function (commit `ee2e6f10c`) surfaces `result_data` to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`. AA does not call `task-status` and does not call `complete_task`.
- **DataProvider surface**: required `load_timeline`, `save_timeline`, `load_asset_registry`, `resolve_asset_url`. Optional `register_asset`, `upload_asset`, `load_checkpoints`, `save_checkpoint`, `load_waveform`, `load_asset_profile`. Explicitly NOT present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile`.
- **JWKS module name**: `artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py` — mirrors the reference `banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` so future readers can map between AA and the reference image.

Repository shape that matters (additions in v5 marked):
- `package.json` (**new at repo root**, `file:` deps to package roots), `scripts/node/{ops_helper.mjs,export_fixtures.mjs}` (**new** — `sequence_helper.mjs` removed).
- `remotion/src/Root.tsx`, `remotion/package.json`, `remotion/node_modules/@banodoco/{timeline-composition,timeline-schema}/**`.
- `artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,cli.py,project.py,run.py,source.py}`.
- `artagents/core/reigh/{__init__.py,env.py,errors.py,supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,data_provider.py,task_client.py,worker_jwt.py}` (**new**, no `sequences.py`).
- `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py` (**new**).
- `artagents/timeline.py`, `artagents/packs/builtin/{reigh_data,open_in_reigh}/run.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`.
- `tests/test_project_*.py`, `tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`, `tests/test_reigh_data.py`, `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py`, plus new `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`, `tests/test_worker_jwt.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`. (No `test_sequence_support.py`.)

## Phase 2: Supabase Client + DataProvider Surface

### Step 5: Extract shared env + auth helpers (`artagents/core/reigh/env.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/reigh/env.py`. Move env helpers from `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py:19-105`.
2. **Add** `resolve_supabase_url(...)`, `resolve_claim_url(...)`, `resolve_task_status_update_url(...)`, `resolve_service_role_key(...)` (env: `REIGH_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`), `resolve_jwks_url(...)`.
3. **Update** `artagents/packs/builtin/reigh_data/run.py` to re-export from `artagents.core.reigh.env` so `tests/test_reigh_data.py` keeps passing.

### Step 6: Implement `SupabaseDataProvider` mirroring the canonical TS surface exactly (`artagents/core/reigh/{supabase_client.py,timeline_io.py,errors.py,data_provider.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/errors.py` with `TimelineVersionConflictError` and `TimelineNotFoundError`. (No `UnsupportedSequenceError` — Phase 5 is deferred.)
2. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/supabase_client.py` — thin POST/RPC wrapper. Auth selector parameterised: `auth=("user_jwt", token)`, `auth=("pat", token)`, or `auth=("service_role", key)`. `update_timeline_config_versioned` is called as a Supabase RPC; a version-mismatch result maps to `TimelineVersionConflictError`.
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/timeline_io.py` with the mutator-based save:

   ```python
   def save_timeline(
       *,
       timeline_id: str,
       mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig],
       service_role_key: str,
       retries: int = 3,
       force: bool = False,
   ) -> SaveResult: ...
   ```

   Loop: load `(timeline, config_version)` via `reigh-data-fetch`, apply `mutator`, then call the RPC with the **exact three-arg signature**:

   ```python
   supabase_client.rpc(
       "update_timeline_config_versioned",
       params={
           "p_timeline_id": timeline_id,
           "p_expected_version": config_version,
           "p_config": new_config,
       },
       auth=("service_role", service_role_key),
   )
   ```

   No `project_id` is passed — the RPC takes only `(uuid, integer, jsonb)`. On version-mismatch result, re-fetch + re-apply the mutator, up to `retries`. Reject `expected_version=None` on the normal path; `force=True` is the only bypass and it logs.
4. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/data_provider.py` with `class SupabaseDataProvider` whose method surface matches `data/DataProvider.ts` exactly:
   - **Required**: `load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> tuple[TimelineConfig, int]` (returns `(config, config_version)`), `save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3, force=False)` (wraps `timeline_io.save_timeline`; takes `service_role_key`, NOT a user_jwt — the RPC is called with the service-role client per `worker_writes.py`), `load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id) -> AssetRegistry`, `resolve_asset_url(asset_entry) -> str`.
   - **Optional**: `register_asset(project_id, timeline_id, asset_entry)`, `upload_asset(local_path_or_bytes, *, content_type, user_id, timeline_id, filename) -> AssetEntry` (uploads to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}`, then `register_asset`), `load_checkpoints(...)`, `save_checkpoint(...)`, `load_waveform(asset_id)`, `load_asset_profile(asset_id)`.
   - **Explicitly NOT** present: `save_waveform`, `save_profile`, `load_profile`.
5. **Round-trip** every fetched timeline through `artagents.timeline.validate_timeline` / `load_timeline` / `save_timeline` so allowlist parity stays intact.

### Step 7: Update `open_in_reigh` to push via `SupabaseDataProvider` by default (`artagents/packs/builtin/open_in_reigh/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the default file-copy path with `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator=lambda t, v: merged_timeline, service_role_key=...)`. Keep `--print-sql` and `--copy-files` as opt-in escape hatches.
2. **Update** `tests/test_open_in_reigh.py` to mock the DataProvider on the default path.

## Phase 3: Banodoco Worker (Mirrors banodoco-worker reference image)

### Step 8: Implement task-protocol client and JWKS verifier (`artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py`, `artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py` wrapping the **exact** task endpoints used by `banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130`:
   - `claim_next_task(*, worker_id, service_role_key) -> ClaimResult | None` — POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with body `{worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}`. Returns the SD-034 envelope `{ task_id, run_id, project_id, params, user_jwt, ... }` or `None`. Mirrors `banodoco-worker/worker.py`, NOT the generic `task_utils.py` shape.
   - `update_task_status(task_id, *, status, result_data=None, error=None, service_role_key)` — POSTs to `/functions/v1/update-task-status` with `status ∈ {Queued, In Progress, Complete, Failed, Cancelled}` (Title Case only) and optional `result_data: dict` (persisted into `tasks.result_data`).
2. **Document** in the module docstring that AA never calls `complete_task` (file/storage shape) and never calls `task-status` (that's reigh-app's `pollBanodocoTaskStatus` reader).
3. **Create** `artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py` — verifies a user_jwt against the JWKS published at `resolve_jwks_url()`. Mirrors the reference `banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py`. Used **for authorization only** — confirms the user owns the timeline; the DB write itself uses service-role.

### Step 9: Implement worker (`artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** `artagents/core/worker/__init__.py` and `artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py`. Module docstring records the resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with citations to `banodoco-worker/worker.py` (claim shape), `banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py` (service-role RPC), `banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` (JWKS authz), and `task-status/index.ts` (commit `ee2e6f10c`).
2. **Reception**: long-running poll loop calling `task_client.claim_next_task(worker_id=AA_WORKER_ID, service_role_key=...)`. AA defensively rejects any envelope whose `task_type` is not `banodoco_timeline_generate`.
3. **JWKS authorization**: before any timeline write, verify `task.user_jwt` via `worker_jwt.verify(task.user_jwt)`. On failure (signature, expiry, audience, or ownership claim mismatch) the worker calls `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt')` and skips. JWT verification is for **authorization** (proving the user owns the timeline); the DB write uses service-role.
4. **Snapshot fast-path**: if `params.current_timeline` is present, dispatch using it directly (skip `load_timeline`). Otherwise call `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id)`.
5. **Dispatch by `intent`** to existing AA executors. Each executor returns a mutator callable.
6. **Carry-fields**: every emitted clip populates `source_uuid`, `generation`, `pool_id`, `clip_order` (SD-003).
7. **Local provenance**: write `runs/<run_id>.json` with `metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload))` (SD-008).
8. **Timeline write**: `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id=task.params.timeline_id, mutator=executor_mutator, [REDACTED], retries=3)`. Internally this calls the three-arg `update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config)` RPC via the service-role Supabase client. `expected_version=task.params.expected_version` is required; `force=True` is forbidden on this path.
9. **Status report on success**: `task_client.update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': params.timeline_id}, service_role_key=...)`. The local `task-status` GET reader surfaces these fields to `pollBanodocoTaskStatus`.
10. **Status report on failure**: `update_task_status(task_id, status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id})`. Title Case only.
11. **Wire** `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into `artagents/pipeline.py:main` and `_print_entrypoint_help`.

## Phase 4: CLI Collapse — Operate on reigh-app Project UUIDs

### Step 10: Delete the placement schema (`artagents/core/project/{schema.py,materialize.py,timeline.py,project.py}`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Delete** `artagents/core/project/timeline.py` and `artagents/core/project/materialize.py`.
2. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/schema.py`: remove placement symbols. Keep project/source/run schema constants.
3. **Trim** `artagents/core/project/project.py`: stop writing `timeline.json` from `create_project`; delete `load_project_timeline`/`save_project_timeline`. `show_project` either fetches via `SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline` (if `project.json#project_id` is set) or reports cache-only state.
4. **Keep** `source.py`, `run.py`, `paths.py`, `jsonio.py`. Add an opaque `project_id` field on `project.json`.

### Step 11: Rewrite the `projects` CLI surface using direct timeline-ops primitives (`artagents/core/project/cli.py`, `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `artagents/core/project/cli.py` so subcommands are: `create`, `show`, `source add`, `list <project_id>`, `edit <project_id>`. Drop placement verbs.
2. **Create** `scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` (Node ESM) that imports **direct primitives** from the local `@banodoco/timeline-ops` (file: package root): `import { addClip, moveClip, setTimelineTheme } from '@banodoco/timeline-ops';`. Reads `{timeline, version, op, args}` JSON from stdin, dispatches by `op` to the right primitive, applies it, and writes the resulting timeline to stdout. **No** `createTimelineCommandRunner` (it doesn't exist in the local package).
3. **Wire** edit verbs (`add-clip`, `move-clip`, `set-theme`) so they shell to `node scripts/node/ops_helper.mjs` and use the resulting timeline as `mutator`'s return value inside `SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3)`. `expected_version` (read from `reigh-data-fetch`'s `config_version`) is required.
4. **Update** `artagents/core/executor/cli.py:_cmd_run` so `--project` accepts a reigh-app UUID and writes back via `SupabaseDataProvider`. Keep the slug path as cache-only/offline mode.

### Step 12: Rewrite project-CLI tests (`tests/test_project_cli.py`, `tests/test_project_materialize.py`, `tests/test_project_schema.py`, `tests/test_project_runs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `tests/test_project_cli.py::test_projects_cli_*` against mocked `reigh-data-fetch` + `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC (force a version-mismatch and assert the mutator is re-applied; assert exhaustion at N=3; assert that omitting `expected_version` raises rather than force-saving; assert the RPC is called with exactly `p_timeline_id`/`p_expected_version`/`p_config` and a service-role auth header).
2. **Delete** `tests/test_project_materialize.py` (placement-only).
3. **Trim** `tests/test_project_schema.py` to project/source/run schema only.
4. **Extend** `tests/test_project_runs.py` to assert `metadata.baseline_snapshot` is a sha256 hex string at exactly that path.

## Phase 5: Renderer Parity & Coverage Tests

### Step 13: New unit tests (`tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`, `tests/test_task_client.py`, `tests/test_worker_jwt.py`, `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py`: mock the Supabase client. Assert (a) `load_timeline` returns `(config, config_version)` and round-trips via `artagents.timeline`, (b) `save_timeline` re-applies the mutator on RPC version-mismatch and exhausts at N=3, (c) `save_timeline` raises when `expected_version` is omitted unless `force=True`, (d) the RPC is called with **exactly** `{p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config}` (no `project_id` parameter) and the auth header is the **service-role** key, never a user_jwt, (e) `upload_asset` writes to `timeline-assets/${userId}/${timelineId}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then calls `register_asset`, (f) the DataProvider surface is exactly the canonical set (presence + absence tests, including absence of `save_waveform`/`save_profile`/`load_profile`).
2. **Add** `tests/test_task_client.py`: mock `urllib.request`. Assert `claim_next_task` POSTs to `/functions/v1/claim-next-task` with body containing `worker_id`, `run_type='banodoco-worker'`, `worker_pool='banodoco'`, `task_types=['banodoco_timeline_generate']`; `update_task_status` POSTs to `/functions/v1/update-task-status` with **Title Case** statuses and `result_data` as a structured dict; `complete_task` is **never** invoked from anywhere in AA; `task-status` is **never** called by AA.
3. **Add** `tests/test_worker_jwt.py`: assert valid JWTs verify against a fake JWKS; expired/wrong-audience/missing-ownership-claim JWTs raise. The verification is independent of the DB write.
4. **Add** `tests/test_banodoco_worker.py`: feed the worker a fake claim response. Assert (a) JWKS verification gates the write — invalid jwt → `Failed`, (b) intent dispatch, (c) snapshot fast-path with and without `current_timeline`, (d) on success: `update_task_status(status='Complete', result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id})`, (e) on failure: `status='Failed'` with `error` and `result_data={correlation_id}`, (f) carry-fields on emitted clips, (g) `metadata.baseline_snapshot` location, (h) the RPC is invoked with the **service-role** key (not the task's user_jwt).

### Step 14: Renderer parity smoke (`tests/test_renderer_parity.py`, `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs` — Node ESM that reads fixture timelines from `tests/fixtures/sprint08/` (JSON files snapshotted once from `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/testing.ts` via a one-shot `tsx` invocation against the reigh-app source tree). The local `@banodoco/timeline-schema` package does not export those fixtures; the JSON snapshot is the integration point.
2. **Add** `tests/test_renderer_parity.py`: shells to `node scripts/node/export_fixtures.mjs`, renders via AA's Remotion entry headless, compares hash to `tests/fixtures/sprint08/golden/`. Skip with a documented reason if the helper or goldens are unavailable.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** — contract resolution + REQUIRED `config_version` PR + root `package.json` with `file:` deps to package roots.
2. **Phase 1** — renderer swap.
3. **Phase 2** — DataProvider with the canonical method surface, mutator-based retry against the three-arg `update_timeline_config_versioned` RPC via service-role client, asset upload.
4. **Phase 3** — task-protocol client + JWKS authorizer + worker (mirrors `banodoco-worker` reference image module-by-module).
5. **Phase 4** — CLI collapse + node ops helper using direct timeline-ops primitives.
6. **Phase 5** — parity + new unit tests. (No sequence-support phase; deferred.)

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npm --prefix remotion run smoke` and a manual headless render of `examples/hype.timeline.full.json`.
2. After Phase 2: `pytest tests/test_supabase_data_provider.py tests/test_reigh_data.py tests/test_open_in_reigh.py tests/test_timeline_roundtrip_fixture.py`.
3. After Phase 3: `pytest tests/test_task_client.py tests/test_worker_jwt.py tests/test_banodoco_worker.py` plus a manual end-to-end `claim → JWKS authz → service-role RPC → update-task-status(Complete, result_data) → reigh-app pollBanodocoTaskStatus` flow against staging.
4. After Phase 4: `pytest tests/test_project_cli.py tests/test_project_runs.py tests/test_project_schema.py`.
5. Final: full `pytest` and `tests/test_renderer_parity.py` against the sprint-08 golden bank.

For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.
